# Pianoverse Backend and "New song" Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Record or upload a song in the app, have a backend run the engine on it, and see the result in the library and the player — first fully on the Mac (SQLite, local files, a worker thread), then on Modal + Supabase with the web app on Cloudflare Pages.

**Architecture:** A FastAPI server lives inside the `engine/` package (`pianoverse_engine.server`) and calls `run_pipeline` directly. It talks to three plugs — `Store` (SQLAlchemy Core: SQLite locally, Supabase Postgres in the cloud), `Blobs` (a folder locally, Supabase Storage in the cloud) and `Runner` (one worker thread locally, a Modal GPU function in the cloud) — so the API code never changes between environments. The React app gets an API client, reads the library from the server when `VITE_API_URL` is set (otherwise it keeps today's static demo), and gains the "New song" (record / upload) and "Processing" screens from the approved mockups.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11 + uv, FastAPI, uvicorn, SQLAlchemy 2 Core, python-multipart, httpx, psycopg (cloud), Modal (cloud); React 19 + Vite 8 + TypeScript 7, react-router 8, Zustand 5, Vitest + Testing Library, Playwright; pnpm; Cloudflare Pages.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-09-backend-design.md` (binding for this plan), on top of `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-07-pianoverse-design.md` §4, §5.1, §6.1, §7, §9 steps 4–5. Mockups: the approved canvas, artboards `Record` and `Processing` (screens 2 and 3 of spec §6.2).

## Global Constraints

- **All code is in English**: identifiers, comments, docstrings, log lines, test names, engine error/warning `message` strings, CLI help, config. Greek appears **only** as app UI copy (`app/src/i18n/messages.ts` `el`, and the localized note/chord names in `app/src/i18n/music.ts`). Comments explain *why*, never *what*; few of them.
- Python: `engine/` is a uv project, Python 3.11; run everything from `engine/` with `uv run …`; add deps with `uv add` (dev: `uv add --dev`). Fast tests (`uv run pytest`) never load ML models and never touch the network.
- JS: `app/` uses **pnpm only** (never npm or npx; `pnpm dlx` is allowed), Node ≥ 22.22; TypeScript `strict` + `verbatimModuleSyntax` (`import type`), no `enum`s, no constructor parameter properties. Fast tests (`pnpm test`) never touch the network and their output is pristine.
- API contract (spec §4): everything under `/api`; JSON keys camelCase; errors `{"error": {"code", "message"}}` where `code` is stable and `message` is an English fallback; the app always translates by `code`.
- Without `VITE_API_URL` the app behaves exactly as on `main` today (static demo songs, no "+ New"); existing tests and the demo e2e keep passing unchanged.
- Every user-visible app string comes from `messages.ts` in both `el` and `en`. Colours and fonts only via the theme tokens (CSS variables from `src/styles/theme.ts`, values in spec §6). Touch targets ≥ 44 px; icon-only buttons have `aria-label`; icons are inline SVG with `aria-hidden="true"`; no emoji.
- No secrets in git: `engine/.env.local`, `app/.env.local` and `engine/data/` are gitignored.
- Each task ends with a commit whose message ends with the line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

### Rulings against the spec (recorded here so reviewers judge them as requirements)

- **Polling hook instead of TanStack Query** (spec §5.4): one list and one job at a time; a 30-line `usePoll` is enough. Revisit when the library grows caching needs.
- **`GET /songs/{id}/audio` is not built** (spec §4.2): the player has no original-audio mode yet (deferred in the player plan); the uploaded audio is still stored as a blob for later.
- **Stale-job rule only on Modal**: spec §4 applies "no update for 20′ → interrupted" everywhere; locally a job waiting in the queue or a slow Demucs stage on a Mac CPU can exceed 20′, so the local runner relies on startup recovery only.
- **No `_redirects` file for Cloudflare Pages**: Pages already serves the SPA shell for unknown paths when there is no `404.html`, and `/* /index.html 200` is rejected as an infinite loop; Task 11 verifies a deep link instead.
- **Failed jobs are listed with active ones** (`GET /api/jobs?active=1`) and can be dismissed (`DELETE /api/jobs/{id}`), so the library can show the failure instead of a card that silently vanishes.
- **Engine messages switch to English** (main spec §6.1 said Greek fallback): user decision 2026-11-09 — code is English; the app already translates by `code`.
- **Tasks 10–11 need the user** to create accounts and keys (Supabase, Modal, Cloudflare). The controller relays the USER STEPS blocks in Greek, waits, then continues; nothing in Tasks 1–9 depends on them.

```python
def test_probe_duration_reads_the_header(tmp_path):
    src = tmp_path / "in.wav"
    sf.write(src, _sine(12, 22050), 22050)
    assert probe_duration(src) == pytest.approx(12.0, abs=0.01)


def test_probe_duration_decodes_streamed_webm_without_a_duration_header(tmp_path):
    wav = tmp_path / "in.wav"
    sf.write(wav, _sine(11, 48000), 48000)
    # Writing to a pipe is how MediaRecorder-style webm ends up without a duration header.
    webm = subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-i", str(wav), "-c:a", "libopus", "-f", "webm", "pipe:1"],
                          capture_output=True, check=True).stdout
    src = tmp_path / "rec.webm"
    src.write_bytes(webm)
    assert probe_duration(src) == pytest.approx(11.0, abs=0.1)


def test_probe_duration_rejects_non_audio(tmp_path):
    src = tmp_path / "in.mp3"
    src.write_bytes(b"definitely not audio")
    with pytest.raises(AudioError) as err:
        probe_duration(src)
    assert err.value.code == "unsupported_format"
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `probe_duration` and `check_duration`**

Replace `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/audio.py` with:

```python
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import librosa
import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf

from .errors import AudioError

TARGET_SR = 44100
MIN_SEC = 10
MAX_SEC = 480
SILENCE_RMS = 1e-3


def _require_ffmpeg() -> None:
    if shutil.which("ffmpeg") is None or shutil.which("ffprobe") is None:
        raise AudioError("ffmpeg_missing", "ffmpeg is not installed on this system.")


def _unreadable() -> AudioError:
    return AudioError("unsupported_format", "Could not read this audio file.")


def _decoded_duration(path: Path) -> float:
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["ffmpeg", "-v", "error", "-nostats", "-i", str(path), "-map", "0:a:0",
         "-f", "null", "-", "-progress", "pipe:1"],
        capture_output=True, text=True,
    )
    times = [line.removeprefix("out_time_us=") for line in result.stdout.splitlines()
             if line.startswith("out_time_us=")]
    if result.returncode != 0 or not times or not times[-1].isdigit():
        raise _unreadable()
    return int(times[-1]) / 1_000_000


def probe_duration(path: Path) -> float:
    """Duration of an audio file in seconds, read from its header when it has one."""
    _require_ffmpeg()
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-show_entries", "format=duration",
         "-of", "default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1", str(path)],
        capture_output=True, text=True,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise _unreadable()
    try:
        duration = float(result.stdout.strip())
    except ValueError:
        # Browser recordings (MediaRecorder webm) are streamed and carry no duration header.
        duration = _decoded_duration(path)
    if not duration > 0:
        raise _unreadable()
    return duration


def check_duration(duration: float) -> None:
    if duration < MIN_SEC:
        raise AudioError("too_short", "The recording is too short (at least 10 seconds are needed).")
    if duration > MAX_SEC:
        raise AudioError("too_long", "The song is too long (8 minutes at most).")


def normalize(src: Path, dst: Path) -> float:
    """Convert any audio file to 44.1 kHz stereo wav; return its duration."""
    _require_ffmpeg()
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-v", "error", "-i", str(src),
         "-ac", "2", "-ar", str(TARGET_SR), "-c:a", "pcm_s16le", str(dst)],
        capture_output=True, text=True,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise _unreadable()
    data, sr = sf.read(dst, always_2d=True, dtype="float32")
    duration = len(data) / sr
    check_duration(duration)
    if float(np.sqrt(np.mean(data ** 2))) < SILENCE_RMS:
        raise AudioError("silent", "Nothing is audible in the recording.")
    return duration


def load_mono(path: Path, sr: int | None = None) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
    data, file_sr = sf.read(path, always_2d=True, dtype="float32")
    y = data.mean(axis=1)
    if sr is not None and sr != file_sr:
        y = librosa.resample(y, orig_sr=file_sr, target_sr=sr)
        return y.astype(np.float32), sr
    return y, file_sr
```

Why the fallback: Chrome's `MediaRecorder` streams webm, so the file has no duration in its header and `ffprobe` prints `N/A`; decoding to the null muxer and reading the last `out_time_us` gives the real length (about a second for 8 minutes of audio).

```python
def test_progress_stages_and_preview_arrive_in_order(tmp_path, song_file):
    events: list[tuple[str, float] | Preview] = []
    song = run_pipeline(song_file, tmp_path / "work", title="Test", engines=FakeEngines().engines(),
                        progress=lambda stage, fraction: events.append((stage, fraction)),
                        on_preview=events.append)
    previews = [e for e in events if isinstance(e, Preview)]
    assert [e if isinstance(e, tuple) else "preview" for e in events] == [
        ("separating", 0.05), ("analyzing", 0.35), "preview", ("melody", 0.6), ("arranging", 0.85), ("done", 1.0),
    ]
    assert previews == [Preview(key=song.key, bpm=song.tempo.bpm, timeSignature=song.timeSignature,
                                chords=["C", "Am", "F", "G"])]


def test_preview_chords_are_the_first_four_distinct_symbols_without_no_chord():
    symbols = ["N", "C", "C", "Am", "N", "C", "F", "G", "Em"]
    segments = [ChordSegment(start=i, end=i + 1, symbol=s, root=s[0], quality="maj") for i, s in enumerate(symbols)]
    assert preview_chords(segments) == ["C", "Am", "F", "G"]
    assert preview_chords(segments[:3]) == ["C"]
```

```python
def preview_chords(chords: list[ChordSegment], limit: int = PREVIEW_CHORDS) -> list[str]:
    """The first `limit` distinct chord symbols, skipping no-chord ("N")."""
    out: list[str] = []
    for chord in chords:
        if chord.symbol != "N" and chord.symbol not in out:
            out.append(chord.symbol)
            if len(out) == limit:
                break
    return out
```

- replace the whole `run_pipeline` function with:

```python
def run_pipeline(
    src: Path, workdir: Path, *, title: str, mode: Mode = "auto", song_id: str | None = None,
    engines: Engines | None = None, progress: ProgressFn = _no_progress,
    on_preview: PreviewFn | None = None,
) -> Song:
    engines = engines or default_engines()
    workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    norm = workdir / "input.wav"
    duration = normalize(src, norm)
    if engines.prepare is not None:
        engines.prepare()

    progress("separating", 0.05)
    stems = engines.separate(norm)
    stem_paths = stems.save(workdir / "stems")
    energies = stems.energies()
    source_type = classify(energies) if mode == "auto" else mode

    progress("analyzing", 0.35)
    beats, downbeats = engines.track_beats(norm)
    if len(beats) < 4:
        raise PipelineError("no_beats", "No steady beat found in the song.")
    tempo = tempo_from_beats(beats, downbeats)
    meter = time_signature(beats, downbeats)
    chords, uncertain = engines.recognize_chords(stems, beats, duration)
    key = estimate_key(chroma_profile(stems.mix(HARMONIC_STEMS), stems.sr))
    if on_preview is not None:
        on_preview(Preview(key=key, bpm=tempo.bpm, timeSignature=meter, chords=preview_chords(chords)))

    progress("melody", 0.6)
    full_piano: list[RawNote] = []
    if source_type == "solo_piano":
        full_piano = clean_notes(engines.transcribe_piano(norm))
        raw = full_piano
    elif source_type == "vocal":
        raw = drop_ghost_notes(clean_notes(engines.transcribe_polyphonic(stem_paths["vocals"])))
    else:
        raw = drop_ghost_notes(clean_notes(engines.transcribe_polyphonic(stem_paths[lead_stem(energies)])))
    melody = skyline(raw)
    if not melody:
        raise PipelineError("no_melody", "Could not find a melody in the song.")

    progress("arranging", 0.85)
    args = (melody, chords, beats, downbeats, duration)
    arrangements: dict[str, Arrangement] = {
        "easy": arrange_easy(*args), "medium": arrange_medium(*args), "hard": arrange_hard(*args),
    }
    if full_piano:
        arrangements["original"] = arrange_original(full_piano)

    song = Song(
        id=song_id or uuid.uuid4().hex[:12],
        title=title,
        durationSec=round(duration, 3),
        sourceType=source_type,
        tempo=tempo,
        timeSignature=meter,
        key=key,
        chords=chords,
        arrangements=arrangements,
        warnings=_warnings(melody, duration, uncertain),
    )
    progress("done", 1.0)
    return song
```

(Tempo and meter are computed right after beat tracking so the preview can carry them; the `Song` reuses the same values.)

- [ ] **Step 13: Print the preview from the CLI**

Replace `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/cli.py` with:

```python
import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from .errors import EngineError
from .export import write_outputs
from .models import Preview
from .pipeline import run_pipeline


def _print_preview(preview: Preview) -> None:
    beats, unit = preview.timeSignature
    print(f"found: {preview.key.tonic} {preview.key.mode} · {preview.bpm:.0f} BPM · {beats}/{unit} · "
          f"{' '.join(preview.chords)}", file=sys.stderr)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="pianoverse-engine",
                                     description="Song → piano arrangement")
    parser.add_argument("input", type=Path, help="audio file (mp3, wav, m4a, …)")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True, help="output folder")
    parser.add_argument("--title", help="title (default: file name)")
    parser.add_argument("--mode", default="auto",
                        choices=["auto", "vocal", "instrumental", "solo_piano"])
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    def progress(stage: str, fraction: float) -> None:
        print(f"[{fraction:4.0%}] {stage}", file=sys.stderr)

    try:
        song = run_pipeline(args.input, args.out / "work", title=args.title or args.input.stem,
                            mode=args.mode, progress=progress, on_preview=_print_preview)
    except EngineError as err:
        print(f"Error ({err.code}): {err.message}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    write_outputs(song, args.out)
    print(args.out / "song.json")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `Settings`**

Create an empty `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/__init__.py`, then `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/config.py`:

```python
import os
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, cast

ENGINE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
LOCALHOST = "127.0.0.1"
RUNNERS = ("local", "modal")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    api_key: str | None
    data_dir: Path
    database_url: str
    cors_origins: list[str]
    runner: Literal["local", "modal"]
    host: str
    port: int
    supabase_url: str | None
    supabase_service_key: str | None
    max_upload_bytes: int = 50 * 1024 * 1024
    stale_after_sec: int = 1200

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls, env: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> "Settings":
        def get(name: str) -> str | None:
            return env.get(name, "").strip() or None

        api_key = get("PIANOVERSE_API_KEY")
        host = get("PIANOVERSE_HOST") or LOCALHOST
        if api_key is None and host != LOCALHOST:
            raise ValueError("PIANOVERSE_API_KEY is required when PIANOVERSE_HOST is not 127.0.0.1")
        runner = get("PIANOVERSE_RUNNER") or "local"
        if runner not in RUNNERS:
            raise ValueError(f"PIANOVERSE_RUNNER must be one of {', '.join(RUNNERS)}")
        data_dir = Path(get("PIANOVERSE_DATA_DIR") or ENGINE_DIR / "data")
        origins = get("PIANOVERSE_CORS_ORIGINS") or "http://localhost:5173"
        return cls(
            api_key=api_key,
            data_dir=data_dir,
            database_url=get("PIANOVERSE_DATABASE_URL") or f"sqlite:///{data_dir / 'pianoverse.db'}",
            cors_origins=[o.strip() for o in origins.split(",") if o.strip()],
            runner=cast(Literal["local", "modal"], runner),
            host=host,
            port=int(get("PIANOVERSE_PORT") or 8000),
            supabase_url=get("SUPABASE_URL"),
            supabase_service_key=get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY"),
        )
```

```python
"""Store contract: every backend must pass these. Postgres joins the `store` fixture's params later."""
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pytest

from pianoverse_engine.server.store import SongRecord, Store


@pytest.fixture(params=["sqlite"])
def store(request, tmp_path) -> Store:
    return Store(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'test.db'}")


def _job(store: Store, title: str = "Song"):
    return store.create_job(title=title, mode="auto", source="file", duration_sec=30.5,
                            audio_key="audio/x.m4a")


def _song(song_id: str, created_at: datetime) -> SongRecord:
    return SongRecord(id=song_id, title=f"Song {song_id}", created_at=created_at, duration_sec=200.0,
                      source_type="vocal", key_tonic="A", key_mode="minor", bpm=92.5,
                      chords=["Am", "F", "C", "G"])


def test_create_job_starts_queued(store):
    job = _job(store)
    assert len(job.id) == 12
    assert (job.status, job.progress, job.attempts) == ("queued", 0.0, 0)
    assert (job.preview, job.song_id, job.error_code, job.error_message) == (None, None, None, None)
    assert job.created_at.tzinfo is not None
    assert store.get_job(job.id) == job


def test_get_missing_job_is_none(store):
    assert store.get_job("nope") is None


def test_update_job_sets_fields_and_bumps_updated_at(store):
    job = _job(store)
    preview = {"key": {"tonic": "A", "mode": "minor"}, "bpm": 90.0, "timeSignature": [4, 4],
               "chords": ["Am", "F"]}
    store.update_job(job.id, status="analyzing", progress=0.35, preview=preview, attempts=1)
    got = store.get_job(job.id)
    assert (got.status, got.progress, got.preview, got.attempts) == ("analyzing", 0.35, preview, 1)
    assert got.updated_at > job.updated_at
    assert got.created_at == job.created_at


def test_active_and_failed_lists_are_newest_first(store):
    first, second, third, done = (_job(store, t) for t in ("first", "second", "third", "done"))
    store.update_job(second.id, status="melody")
    store.update_job(third.id, status="failed", error_code="silent", error_message="Nothing is audible.")
    store.update_job(done.id, status="done", progress=1.0, song_id=done.id)
    assert [j.id for j in store.list_active_jobs()] == [second.id, first.id]
    assert [j.id for j in store.list_failed_jobs()] == [third.id]


def test_fail_interrupted_fails_every_active_job(store):
    queued, running, done = _job(store), _job(store), _job(store)
    store.update_job(running.id, status="separating")
    store.update_job(done.id, status="done")
    assert store.fail_interrupted() == 2
    for job_id in (queued.id, running.id):
        got = store.get_job(job_id)
        assert (got.status, got.error_code) == ("failed", "interrupted")
        assert got.error_message
    assert store.get_job(done.id).status == "done"


def test_fail_interrupted_with_cutoff_spares_recent_jobs(store):
    job = _job(store)
    assert store.fail_interrupted(older_than=job.updated_at - timedelta(seconds=1)) == 0
    assert store.get_job(job.id).status == "queued"
    assert store.fail_interrupted(older_than=job.updated_at + timedelta(seconds=1)) == 1
    assert store.get_job(job.id).status == "failed"


def test_dismiss_deletes_only_failed_jobs(store):
    failed, active = _job(store), _job(store)
    store.update_job(failed.id, status="failed", error_code="silent")
    store.dismiss_job(failed.id)
    store.dismiss_job(active.id)
    assert store.get_job(failed.id) is None
    assert store.get_job(active.id) is not None


def test_songs_round_trip_newest_first(store):
    now = datetime.now(UTC).replace(microsecond=0)
    old, new = _song("old", now - timedelta(days=1)), _song("new", now)
    store.create_song(old)
    store.create_song(new)
    assert store.get_song("old") == old
    assert store.get_song("missing") is None
    assert [s.id for s in store.list_songs()] == ["new", "old"]


def test_tables_survive_a_second_store_on_the_same_database(tmp_path):
    url = f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'test.db'}"
    job = _job(Store(url))
    assert Store(url).get_job(job.id) == job
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the Store**

Create `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/store.py`:

```python
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import (
    JSON, Column, DateTime, Float, Integer, MetaData, RowMapping, String, Table, Text,
    create_engine, delete, insert, select, update,
)

ACTIVE_STATUSES = ("queued", "separating", "analyzing", "melody", "arranging")
INTERRUPTED_MESSAGE = "Processing was interrupted. Try again."

metadata = MetaData()

jobs = Table(
    "jobs", metadata,
    Column("id", String(32), primary_key=True),
    Column("title", Text, nullable=False),
    Column("mode", String(16), nullable=False),
    Column("source", String(16), nullable=False),
    Column("duration_sec", Float, nullable=False),
    Column("status", String(16), nullable=False, index=True),
    Column("progress", Float, nullable=False),
    Column("preview", JSON, nullable=True),
    Column("song_id", String(32), nullable=True),
    Column("error_code", String(64), nullable=True),
    Column("error_message", Text, nullable=True),
    Column("audio_key", Text, nullable=False),
    Column("attempts", Integer, nullable=False),
    Column("created_at", DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),
    Column("updated_at", DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),
)

songs = Table(
    "songs", metadata,
    Column("id", String(32), primary_key=True),
    Column("title", Text, nullable=False),
    Column("created_at", DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),
    Column("duration_sec", Float, nullable=False),
    Column("source_type", String(16), nullable=False),
    Column("key_tonic", String(4), nullable=False),
    Column("key_mode", String(8), nullable=False),
    Column("bpm", Float, nullable=False),
    Column("chords", JSON, nullable=False),
)


@dataclass
class JobRecord:
    id: str
    title: str
    mode: str
    source: str
    duration_sec: float
    status: str
    progress: float
    preview: dict[str, Any] | None
    song_id: str | None
    error_code: str | None
    error_message: str | None
    audio_key: str
    attempts: int
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime


@dataclass
class SongRecord:
    id: str
    title: str
    created_at: datetime
    duration_sec: float
    source_type: str
    key_tonic: str
    key_mode: str
    bpm: float
    chords: list[str]


def utcnow() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)


def _utc(value: datetime) -> datetime:
    # SQLite hands back naive datetimes; everything stored here is UTC.
    return value.replace(tzinfo=UTC) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(UTC)


def _job(row: RowMapping) -> JobRecord:
    return JobRecord(**{**row, "created_at": _utc(row["created_at"]), "updated_at": _utc(row["updated_at"])})


def _song(row: RowMapping) -> SongRecord:
    return SongRecord(**{**row, "created_at": _utc(row["created_at"])})


class Store:
    """Job and song metadata. One implementation for SQLite (local) and Postgres (cloud)."""

    def __init__(self, database_url: str):
        connect_args = {"check_same_thread": False} if database_url.startswith("sqlite") else {}
        self._engine = create_engine(database_url, connect_args=connect_args)
        metadata.create_all(self._engine)

    def create_job(self, *, title: str, mode: str, source: str, duration_sec: float,
                   audio_key: str) -> JobRecord:
        now = utcnow()
        job = JobRecord(id=uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], title=title, mode=mode, source=source,
                        duration_sec=duration_sec, status="queued", progress=0.0, preview=None,
                        song_id=None, error_code=None, error_message=None, audio_key=audio_key,
                        attempts=0, created_at=now, updated_at=now)
        with self._engine.begin() as conn:
            conn.execute(insert(jobs).values(**vars(job)))
        return job

    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> JobRecord | None:
        with self._engine.connect() as conn:
            row = conn.execute(select(jobs).where(jobs.c.id == job_id)).mappings().first()
        return _job(row) if row else None

    def list_active_jobs(self) -> list[JobRecord]:
        return self._list_jobs(jobs.c.status.in_(ACTIVE_STATUSES))

    def list_failed_jobs(self) -> list[JobRecord]:
        return self._list_jobs(jobs.c.status == "failed")

    def _list_jobs(self, condition: Any) -> list[JobRecord]:
        query = select(jobs).where(condition).order_by(jobs.c.created_at.desc())
        with self._engine.connect() as conn:
            return [_job(row) for row in conn.execute(query).mappings()]

    def update_job(self, job_id: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
        with self._engine.begin() as conn:
            conn.execute(update(jobs).where(jobs.c.id == job_id).values(**fields, updated_at=utcnow()))

    def fail_interrupted(self, *, older_than: datetime | None = None) -> int:
        condition = jobs.c.status.in_(ACTIVE_STATUSES)
        if older_than is not None:
            condition = condition & (jobs.c.updated_at < older_than)
        query = update(jobs).where(condition).values(
            status="failed", error_code="interrupted", error_message=INTERRUPTED_MESSAGE, updated_at=utcnow())
        with self._engine.begin() as conn:
            return conn.execute(query).rowcount

    def dismiss_job(self, job_id: str) -> None:
        with self._engine.begin() as conn:
            conn.execute(delete(jobs).where((jobs.c.id == job_id) & (jobs.c.status == "failed")))

    def create_song(self, rec: SongRecord) -> None:
        with self._engine.begin() as conn:
            conn.execute(insert(songs).values(**vars(rec)))

    def list_songs(self) -> list[SongRecord]:
        with self._engine.connect() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(select(songs).order_by(songs.c.created_at.desc())).mappings()
            return [_song(row) for row in rows]

    def get_song(self, song_id: str) -> SongRecord | None:
        with self._engine.connect() as conn:
            row = conn.execute(select(songs).where(songs.c.id == song_id)).mappings().first()
        return _song(row) if row else None
```

Notes for the implementer: `check_same_thread=False` is needed because the worker thread and request threads share pooled SQLite connections; `_utc` exists because SQLite returns naive datetimes while Postgres returns aware ones.

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement Blobs and LocalBlobs**

Create `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/blobs.py`:

```python
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol


class Blobs(Protocol):
    def put_bytes(self, key: str, data: bytes, content_type: str) -> None: ...
    def put_file(self, key: str, path: Path, content_type: str) -> None: ...
    def get_bytes(self, key: str) -> bytes: ...
    def download(self, key: str, dest: Path) -> None: ...


class LocalBlobs:
    """Blobs as files under one folder; content types only matter to remote stores."""

    def __init__(self, root: Path):
        self.root = root

    def _path(self, key: str) -> Path:
        path = (self.root / key).resolve()
        if not path.is_relative_to(self.root.resolve()):
            raise ValueError(f"blob key escapes the store: {key}")
        return path

    def _existing(self, key: str) -> Path:
        path = self._path(key)
        if not path.is_file():
            raise KeyError(key)
        return path

    def put_bytes(self, key: str, data: bytes, content_type: str) -> None:
        path = self._path(key)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_bytes(data)

    def put_file(self, key: str, path: Path, content_type: str) -> None:
        dest = self._path(key)
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(path, dest)

    def get_bytes(self, key: str) -> bytes:
        return self._existing(key).read_bytes()

    def download(self, key: str, dest: Path) -> None:
        source = self._existing(key)
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile(source, dest)
```

**Files:**

- Test: `engine/tests/test_worker.py`, `engine/tests/test_runner.py`, `engine/tests/test_api_jobs.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `Preview`, `preview_chords`, `run_pipeline(..., progress=, on_preview=)`, `probe_duration`, `check_duration`; Task 2 `Settings`, `Store`, `JobRecord`, `SongRecord`, `ACTIVE_STATUSES`, `utcnow`, `Blobs`, `LocalBlobs`; existing `export.arrangement_to_midi`, `errors.EngineError`/`AudioError`.
- Produces:
  - `server.worker`: `RunFn = Callable[..., Song]`; `INTERNAL_MESSAGE: str`; `song_key(song_id: str, name: str) -> str` (`"songs/{id}/{name}"`); `process_job(job_id: str, store: Store, blobs: Blobs, *, run: RunFn = run_pipeline, workdir_root: Path | None = None) -> None`. Skips jobs that are missing or not in `ACTIVE_STATUSES`; bumps `attempts`; writes each stage + progress, the preview (`Preview.model_dump(mode="json")`), `songs/{id}/song.json`, `songs/{id}/{level}.mid`, a `SongRecord` (chords = `preview_chords`), then `status="done"`, `progress=1.0`, `song_id`. `EngineError` → `failed` + its code/message; anything else → `failed` + `internal` + `log.exception`.
  - `server.runner`: `class Runner(Protocol): submit(job_id: str) -> None`; `class LocalRunner(store, blobs, *, run: RunFn = run_pipeline)` with `start()`, `stop(timeout=5.0)`, `submit(job_id)`, `join_idle(timeout=10.0) -> bool`.
  - `server.api`: `ApiError(status, code, message)`; `job_json(job: JobRecord) -> dict`; `iso(dt) -> str` (`...Z`); `create_app(settings, store, blobs, runner, *, probe=probe_duration, lifespan=None) -> FastAPI`; `app_from_env(env: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> FastAPI` (local runner only for now; lifespan runs `store.fail_interrupted()` then `runner.start()`, and `runner.stop()` on shutdown). Routes: `GET /api/health`, `POST /api/jobs`, `GET /api/jobs`, `GET /api/jobs/{id}`, `DELETE /api/jobs/{id}`. Error codes added beyond the contract: `bad_request` (400, missing/invalid form fields), `ffmpeg_missing` (500).

```python
"""A stand-in for run_pipeline that needs no audio or models."""
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from helpers import make_song
from pianoverse_engine.models import Key, Preview, Song

PREVIEW = Preview(key=Key(tonic="C", mode="major"), bpm=120.0, timeSignature=(4, 4), chords=["C"])
STAGES = [("separating", 0.05), ("analyzing", 0.35), ("melody", 0.6), ("arranging", 0.85)]


def fake_run(src: Path, workdir: Path, *, title: str, mode: str, song_id: str,
             progress: Any, on_preview: Any, **_: Any) -> Song:
    """Reports progress and the preview the way run_pipeline does, then returns helpers.make_song."""
    for stage, fraction in STAGES[:2]:
        progress(stage, fraction)
    on_preview(PREVIEW)
    for stage, fraction in STAGES[2:]:
        progress(stage, fraction)
    progress("done", 1.0)
    return make_song(id=song_id, title=title)
```

```python
from pianoverse_engine.errors import PipelineError
from pianoverse_engine.models import Song

@pytest.fixture

                           audio_key="audio/j.webm")
    blobs.put_bytes("audio/j.webm", b"recorded-audio", "audio/webm")
    return job


def test_success_stores_song_midi_and_record(store, blobs, job, tmp_path):
    seen = {}

    def run(src, workdir, **kwargs):
        seen.update(kwargs, audio=src.read_bytes(), suffix=src.suffix)
        return fake_run(src, workdir, **kwargs)

    process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=run, workdir_root=tmp_path)
    assert (seen["audio"], seen["suffix"]) == (b"recorded-audio", ".webm")
    assert (seen["title"], seen["mode"], seen["song_id"]) == ("My song", "vocal", job.id)

    done = store.get_job(job.id)
    assert (done.status, done.progress, done.song_id, done.attempts) == ("done", 1.0, job.id, 1)
    assert done.preview == {"key": {"tonic": "C", "mode": "major"}, "bpm": 120.0,
                            "timeSignature": [4, 4], "chords": ["C"]}
    song = Song.model_validate_json(blobs.get_bytes(f"songs/{job.id}/song.json"))
    assert song == make_song(id=job.id, title="My song")
    assert "bass" not in json.loads(blobs.get_bytes(f"songs/{job.id}/song.json"))["chords"][0]
    midi_path = tmp_path / "easy.mid"
    midi_path.write_bytes(blobs.get_bytes(f"songs/{job.id}/easy.mid"))
    assert [len(i.notes) for i in pretty_midi.PrettyMIDI(str(midi_path)).instruments] == [1, 1]
    record = store.get_song(job.id)
    assert (record.title, record.source_type, record.key_tonic, record.key_mode) == ("My song", "vocal", "C", "major")
    assert (record.duration_sec, record.bpm, record.chords) == (8.0, 120.0, ["C"])


def test_each_stage_is_written_while_running(store, blobs, job):
    snapshots = []

    def run(src, workdir, *, progress, on_preview, **kwargs):
        for stage, fraction in STAGES:
            progress(stage, fraction)
            current = store.get_job(job.id)
            snapshots.append((current.status, current.progress))
        on_preview(PREVIEW)
        progress("done", 1.0)
        current = store.get_job(job.id)
        snapshots.append((current.status, current.progress))
        return make_song(id=kwargs["song_id"])

    process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=run)
    assert snapshots == STAGES + [("arranging", 0.85)]   # "done" waits for the stored outputs


def test_engine_error_fails_the_job_with_its_code(store, blobs, job):
    def run(*args, **kwargs):
        raise PipelineError("no_melody", "Could not find a melody in the song.")

    process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=run)
    failed = store.get_job(job.id)
    assert (failed.status, failed.error_code, failed.error_message) == (
        "failed", "no_melody", "Could not find a melody in the song.")
    assert store.get_song(job.id) is None


def test_unexpected_error_fails_as_internal_and_logs(store, blobs, job, caplog):
    def run(*args, **kwargs):
        raise RuntimeError("boom")

    with caplog.at_level(logging.ERROR):
        process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=run)
    failed = store.get_job(job.id)
    assert (failed.status, failed.error_code) == ("failed", "internal")
    assert "RuntimeError: boom" in caplog.text


def test_missing_audio_fails_as_internal(store, blobs):
    job = store.create_job(title="x", mode="auto", source="file", duration_sec=30.0, audio_key="audio/none.m4a")
    process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=fake_run)
    assert store.get_job(job.id).error_code == "internal"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status", ["done", "failed"])
def test_finished_jobs_are_not_run_again(store, blobs, job, status):
    store.update_job(job.id, status=status)
    calls = []
    process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=lambda *a, **k: calls.append(1))
    assert calls == []
    assert store.get_job(job.id).attempts == 0


def test_unknown_job_is_ignored(store, blobs):
    process_job("missing", store, blobs, run=fake_run)


def test_workdir_is_removed_afterwards(store, blobs, job, tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "work"
    root.mkdir()
    process_job(job.id, store, blobs, run=fake_run, workdir_root=root)
    assert list(root.iterdir()) == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `process_job`**

Create `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/worker.py`:

```python
import io
import logging
import tempfile
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

from ..errors import EngineError
from ..export import arrangement_to_midi
from ..models import Preview, Song
from ..pipeline import preview_chords, run_pipeline
from .blobs import Blobs
from .store import ACTIVE_STATUSES, SongRecord, Store, utcnow

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

RunFn = Callable[..., Song]
INTERNAL_MESSAGE = "Something went wrong while processing the song."


def song_key(song_id: str, name: str) -> str:
    return f"songs/{song_id}/{name}"


def process_job(job_id: str, store: Store, blobs: Blobs, *, run: RunFn = run_pipeline,
                workdir_root: Path | None = None) -> None:
    """Run one job end to end. Shared by the local worker thread and the cloud GPU function."""
    job = store.get_job(job_id)
    if job is None or job.status not in ACTIVE_STATUSES:
        return   # dismissed, already finished, or failed as stale while it waited
    store.update_job(job_id, attempts=job.attempts + 1)

    def progress(stage: str, fraction: float) -> None:
        if stage != "done":   # "done" is written only once the outputs are stored
            store.update_job(job_id, status=stage, progress=fraction)

    def on_preview(preview: Preview) -> None:
        store.update_job(job_id, preview=preview.model_dump(mode="json"))

    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=workdir_root) as tmp:
            src = Path(tmp) / f"source{Path(job.audio_key).suffix}"
            blobs.download(job.audio_key, src)
            song = run(src, Path(tmp) / "work", title=job.title, mode=job.mode, song_id=job_id,
                       progress=progress, on_preview=on_preview)
        _save_song(song, store, blobs)
    except EngineError as err:
        store.update_job(job_id, status="failed", error_code=err.code, error_message=err.message)
        return
    except Exception:
        log.exception("job %s failed", job_id)
        store.update_job(job_id, status="failed", error_code="internal", error_message=INTERNAL_MESSAGE)
        return
    store.update_job(job_id, status="done", progress=1.0, song_id=song.id)


def _save_song(song: Song, store: Store, blobs: Blobs) -> None:
    blobs.put_bytes(song_key(song.id, "song.json"),
                    song.model_dump_json(exclude_none=True).encode(), "application/json")
    for level, arrangement in song.arrangements.items():
        midi = io.BytesIO()
        arrangement_to_midi(arrangement, song.tempo.bpm).write(midi)
        blobs.put_bytes(song_key(song.id, f"{level}.mid"), midi.getvalue(), "audio/midi")
    store.create_song(SongRecord(
        id=song.id, title=song.title, created_at=utcnow(), duration_sec=song.durationSec,
        source_type=song.sourceType, key_tonic=song.key.tonic, key_mode=song.key.mode,
        bpm=song.tempo.bpm, chords=preview_chords(song.chords),
    ))
```

```python
import threading

import pytest

from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store

def store(tmp_path) -> Store:

@pytest.fixture

def _queued(store: Store, blobs: LocalBlobs, title: str) -> str:
    job = store.create_job(title=title, mode="auto", source="file", duration_sec=30.0,
                           audio_key=f"audio/{title}.m4a")
    blobs.put_bytes(job.audio_key, b"audio", "audio/mp4")
    return job.id


def test_runs_submitted_jobs_in_order(store, blobs):
    order: list[str] = []

    def run(src, workdir, **kwargs):
        order.append(kwargs["title"])
        return fake_run(src, workdir, **kwargs)

    runner = LocalRunner(store, blobs, run=run)
    runner.start()
    ids = [_queued(store, blobs, title) for title in ("one", "two", "three")]
    for job_id in ids:
        runner.submit(job_id)
    assert runner.join_idle(10)
    runner.stop()
    assert order == ["one", "two", "three"]
    assert {store.get_job(job_id).status for job_id in ids} == {"done"}


def test_join_idle_times_out_while_a_job_is_running(store, blobs):
    release = threading.Event()

    def run(src, workdir, **kwargs):
        release.wait(5)
        return fake_run(src, workdir, **kwargs)

    runner = LocalRunner(store, blobs, run=run)
    runner.start()
    runner.submit(_queued(store, blobs, "slow"))
    assert runner.join_idle(0.1) is False
    release.set()
    assert runner.join_idle(5) is True
    runner.stop()


def test_stop_is_safe_twice_and_before_start(store, blobs):
    runner = LocalRunner(store, blobs, run=fake_run)
    runner.stop()
    runner.start()
    runner.stop()
    runner.stop()
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the runner**

Create `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/runner.py`:

```python
import logging
import queue
import threading
from typing import Protocol

from ..pipeline import run_pipeline
from .blobs import Blobs
from .store import Store
from .worker import RunFn, process_job

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class Runner(Protocol):
    def submit(self, job_id: str) -> None: ...


class LocalRunner:
    """One background thread that processes jobs in submission order, one at a time."""

    def __init__(self, store: Store, blobs: Blobs, *, run: RunFn = run_pipeline):
        self._store = store
        self._blobs = blobs
        self._run = run
        self._queue: queue.Queue[str | None] = queue.Queue()
        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
        self._pending = 0
        self._idle = threading.Condition()

    def start(self) -> None:
        if self._thread is None:
            self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._loop, name="pianoverse-worker", daemon=True)
            self._thread.start()

    def stop(self, timeout: float = 5.0) -> None:
        if self._thread is not None:
            self._queue.put(None)
            self._thread.join(timeout)
            self._thread = None

    def submit(self, job_id: str) -> None:
        with self._idle:
            self._pending += 1
        self._queue.put(job_id)

    def join_idle(self, timeout: float = 10.0) -> bool:
        """Wait until every submitted job has been processed (for tests)."""
        with self._idle:
            return self._idle.wait_for(lambda: self._pending == 0, timeout)

    def _loop(self) -> None:
        while (job_id := self._queue.get()) is not None:
            try:
                process_job(job_id, self._store, self._blobs, run=self._run)
            except Exception:   # a broken database must not kill the worker thread
                log.exception("worker crashed on job %s", job_id)
            finally:
                with self._idle:
                    self._pending -= 1
                    self._idle.notify_all()
```

```python
"""Shared pieces for the API tests."""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from pianoverse_engine.server.blobs import LocalBlobs
from pianoverse_engine.server.config import Settings
from pianoverse_engine.server.runner import LocalRunner
from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store

API_KEY = "test-key"
AUTH = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {API_KEY}"}


def thirty_seconds(path: Path) -> float:
    """Stands in for ffprobe so uploads need no real audio."""
    return 30.0


def make_settings(tmp_path: Path, **overrides: Any) -> Settings:
    values: dict[str, Any] = dict(
        api_key=API_KEY, data_dir=tmp_path, database_url=f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'test.db'}",
        cors_origins=["http://localhost:5173"], runner="local", host="127.0.0.1", port=8000,
        supabase_url=None, supabase_service_key=None,
    )
    values.update(overrides)
    return Settings(**values)


@dataclass
class Server:
    client: TestClient
    store: Store
    blobs: LocalBlobs
    runner: LocalRunner


def upload(client: TestClient, *, audio: bytes = b"fake-audio", filename: str = "song.m4a",
           headers: dict[str, str] | None = None, **form: str):
    data = {"title": "My song", "mode": "auto", "source": "file"} | form
    return client.post("/api/jobs", headers=AUTH if headers is None else headers,
                       files={"audio": (filename, audio, "application/octet-stream")}, data=data)
```

```python
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from fake_pipeline import fake_run
from pianoverse_engine.server.api import create_app
from pianoverse_engine.server.blobs import LocalBlobs
from pianoverse_engine.server.runner import LocalRunner
from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store
from server_helpers import Server, make_settings, thirty_seconds


@pytest.fixture
def make_server(tmp_path) -> Iterator[Callable[..., Server]]:
    """Build an app on a temp folder with a started LocalRunner; stops every runner afterwards."""
    runners: list[LocalRunner] = []

    def make(*, run: Any = fake_run, probe: Any = thirty_seconds, **settings: Any) -> Server:
        config = make_settings(tmp_path, **settings)
        store = Store(config.database_url)
        blobs = LocalBlobs(tmp_path / "blobs")
        runner = LocalRunner(store, blobs, run=run)
        runner.start()
        runners.append(runner)
        client = TestClient(create_app(config, store, blobs, runner, probe=probe))
        return Server(client=client, store=store, blobs=blobs, runner=runner)

    yield make
    for runner in runners:
        runner.stop()
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the jobs API**

Create `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/api.py`:

```python
import hmac
import os
import re
import tempfile
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator, Callable, Mapping
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Any, BinaryIO

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, FastAPI, File, Form, Header, Request, Response, UploadFile
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse

from ..audio import check_duration, probe_duration
from ..errors import AudioError
from .blobs import Blobs, LocalBlobs
from .config import Settings
from .runner import LocalRunner, Runner
from .store import JobRecord, Store, utcnow
from .worker import INTERNAL_MESSAGE

MODES = ("auto", "vocal", "instrumental", "solo_piano")
SOURCES = ("recording", "file")
CHUNK = 1024 * 1024

Lifespan = Callable[[FastAPI], Any]


class ApiError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status: int, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status
        self.code = code
        self.message = message


def _error(status: int, code: str, message: str) -> JSONResponse:
    return JSONResponse({"error": {"code": code, "message": message}}, status_code=status)


def iso(value: datetime) -> str:
    return value.isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def job_json(job: JobRecord) -> dict[str, Any]:
    out: dict[str, Any] = {
        "id": job.id, "title": job.title, "status": job.status, "progress": job.progress,
        "source": job.source, "durationSec": job.duration_sec, "createdAt": iso(job.created_at),
    }
    if job.song_id:
        out["songId"] = job.song_id
    if job.preview:
        out["preview"] = job.preview
    if job.error_code:
        out["error"] = {"code": job.error_code, "message": job.error_message or ""}
    return out


def _audio_suffix(filename: str | None) -> str:
    suffix = Path(filename or "").suffix.lower()
    return suffix if re.fullmatch(r"\.[a-z0-9]{1,8}", suffix) else ".bin"


def _save_upload(source: BinaryIO, dest: Path, limit: int) -> None:
    size = 0
    with dest.open("wb") as out:
        while chunk := source.read(CHUNK):
            size += len(chunk)
            if size > limit:
                raise ApiError(413, "too_large", "The file is too large.")
            out.write(chunk)


def create_app(settings: Settings, store: Store, blobs: Blobs, runner: Runner, *,
               probe: Callable[[Path], float] = probe_duration, lifespan: Lifespan | None = None) -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(title="Pianoverse", lifespan=lifespan)
    app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=settings.cors_origins,
                       allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "DELETE"], allow_headers=["Authorization"])

    @app.exception_handler(ApiError)
    async def api_error(request: Request, err: ApiError) -> JSONResponse:
        return _error(err.status, err.code, err.message)

    @app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)
    async def bad_request(request: Request, err: RequestValidationError) -> JSONResponse:
        return _error(400, "bad_request", "The request is missing fields or has invalid ones.")

    def require_key(authorization: Annotated[str | None, Header()] = None) -> None:
        if settings.api_key is None:
            return
        expected = f"Bearer {settings.api_key}".encode()
        if authorization is None or not hmac.compare_digest(authorization.encode(), expected):
            raise ApiError(401, "unauthorized", "Missing or wrong API key.")

    def expire_stale() -> None:
        # Locally a queue wait or a long CPU stage is not a crash, and startup recovery covers restarts.
        if settings.runner != "modal":
            return
        store.fail_interrupted(older_than=utcnow() - timedelta(seconds=settings.stale_after_sec))

    def find_job(job_id: str) -> JobRecord:
        job = store.get_job(job_id)
        if job is None:
            raise ApiError(404, "not_found", "No such job.")
        return job

    @app.get("/api/health")
    def health() -> dict[str, bool]:
        return {"ok": True}

    api = APIRouter(prefix="/api", dependencies=[Depends(require_key)])

    @api.post("/jobs", status_code=202)
    def create_job(
        audio: Annotated[UploadFile, File()],
        title: Annotated[str, Form()] = "",
        mode: Annotated[str, Form()] = "auto",
        source: Annotated[str, Form()] = "file",
    ) -> dict[str, str]:
        if mode not in MODES:
            raise ApiError(400, "bad_mode", f"mode must be one of {', '.join(MODES)}.")
        if source not in SOURCES:
            raise ApiError(400, "bad_source", f"source must be one of {', '.join(SOURCES)}.")
        title = title.strip()
        if not title:
            raise ApiError(400, "bad_title", "The title is empty.")
        suffix = _audio_suffix(audio.filename)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            path = Path(tmp) / f"upload{suffix}"
            _save_upload(audio.file, path, settings.max_upload_bytes)
            try:
                duration = probe(path)
                check_duration(duration)
            except AudioError as err:
                raise ApiError(500 if err.code == "ffmpeg_missing" else 400, err.code, err.message) from err
            job = store.create_job(title=title, mode=mode, source=source,
                                   duration_sec=round(duration, 3), audio_key="")
            audio_key = f"audio/{job.id}{suffix}"
            try:
                blobs.put_file(audio_key, path, audio.content_type or "application/octet-stream")
            except Exception:
                store.update_job(job.id, status="failed", error_code="internal", error_message=INTERNAL_MESSAGE)
                raise
        store.update_job(job.id, audio_key=audio_key)
        runner.submit(job.id)
        return {"jobId": job.id}

    @api.get("/jobs")
    def list_jobs(active: int = 1) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        # Only the unfinished view exists: running jobs plus failures the user has not dismissed.
        expire_stale()
        unfinished = store.list_active_jobs() + store.list_failed_jobs()
        unfinished.sort(key=lambda job: job.created_at, reverse=True)
        return [job_json(job) for job in unfinished]

    @api.get("/jobs/{job_id}")
    def get_job(job_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        expire_stale()
        return job_json(find_job(job_id))

    @api.delete("/jobs/{job_id}", status_code=204)
    def dismiss_job(job_id: str) -> Response:
        if find_job(job_id).status != "failed":
            raise ApiError(409, "not_failed", "Only failed jobs can be dismissed.")
        store.dismiss_job(job_id)
        return Response(status_code=204)

    app.include_router(api)
    return app


def app_from_env(env: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> FastAPI:
    settings = Settings.from_env(env)
    if settings.runner != "local":
        raise ValueError("Only PIANOVERSE_RUNNER=local is available so far")
    settings.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    store = Store(settings.database_url)
    blobs = LocalBlobs(settings.data_dir / "blobs")
    runner = LocalRunner(store, blobs)

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        store.fail_interrupted()   # the in-memory queue died with the previous process
        runner.start()
        yield
        runner.stop()

    return create_app(settings, store, blobs, runner, lifespan=lifespan)
```

Design points the code relies on:
- The job row is created before the audio blob because its id names the blob (`audio/{jobId}{suffix}`); `audio_key` is filled in right after the upload, and only then is the job submitted. A failed blob upload marks the job `failed`/`internal`.
- With `runner=modal`, reading jobs applies the stale rule (`fail_interrupted(older_than=now - stale_after_sec)`); the local runner relies on startup recovery instead, because a job waiting in the local queue or a slow Demucs stage on a Mac CPU can exceed 20 minutes; `process_job` skips jobs that are no longer active, so a job failed as stale while it waited in the local queue is not resurrected.
- `GET /api/jobs` returns the unfinished view (active + failed, newest first) whatever `active` says; the app always sends `active=1`.

```python
import io

import pretty_midi
import pytest

from helpers import make_song
from pianoverse_engine.models import Song
from server_helpers import AUTH, upload


@pytest.fixture
def server(make_server):
    return make_server()


def _finished_song(server, title: str = "My song") -> str:
    job_id = upload(server.client, title=title).json()["jobId"]
    assert server.runner.join_idle(10)
    return job_id


def test_songs_list_summaries_newest_first(server):
    first = _finished_song(server, "First")
    second = _finished_song(server, "Second")
    songs = server.client.get("/api/songs", headers=AUTH).json()
    assert [s["id"] for s in songs] == [second, first]
    assert songs[1] == {"id": first, "title": "First", "key": {"tonic": "C", "mode": "major"}, "bpm": 120.0,
                        "durationSec": 8.0, "sourceType": "vocal", "chords": ["C"]}


def test_song_json_is_served_from_blobs(server):

    response = server.client.get(f"/api/songs/{song_id}", headers=AUTH)
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.headers["content-type"] == "application/json"
```

```python
def song_summary_json(song: SongRecord) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": song.id, "title": song.title, "key": {"tonic": song.key_tonic, "mode": song.key_mode},
        "bpm": song.bpm, "durationSec": song.duration_sec, "sourceType": song.source_type,
        "chords": song.chords,
    }
```

- inside `create_app`, insert right before `    app.include_router(api)`:

```python
    def song_blob(song_id: str, name: str) -> bytes:
        if store.get_song(song_id) is None:
            raise ApiError(404, "not_found", "No such song.")
        try:
            return blobs.get_bytes(song_key(song_id, name))
        except KeyError:
            raise ApiError(404, "not_found", "No such song file.") from None

    @api.get("/songs")
    def list_songs() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [song_summary_json(song) for song in store.list_songs()]

    @api.get("/songs/{song_id}")
    def get_song(song_id: str) -> Response:
        return Response(song_blob(song_id, "song.json"), media_type="application/json")

    @api.get("/songs/{song_id}/midi")
    def get_midi(song_id: str, level: str = "easy") -> Response:
        if level not in LEVELS:
            raise ApiError(404, "not_found", "No such level.")
        return Response(song_blob(song_id, f"{level}.mid"), media_type="audio/midi",
                        headers={"Content-Disposition": f'attachment; filename="{song_id}-{level}.mid"'})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Document the server**

In `engine/README.md`, insert before `## Evaluate`:

````markdown
## Server

A small HTTP API (FastAPI) that accepts uploads, runs the engine on a background worker and serves the finished songs to the app. Locally it keeps everything under `data/` (git-ignored): a SQLite database and a `blobs/` folder with the audio, `song.json` and MIDI files.

```bash
PIANOVERSE_API_KEY=dev-key uv run pianoverse-server
curl http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/health                                   # {"ok":true}
curl -H "Authorization: Bearer dev-key" http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/songs
curl -H "Authorization: Bearer dev-key" -F audio=@song.mp3 -F title="My song" \
     -F mode=auto -F source=file http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/jobs         # {"jobId":"…"}
```

Point the app at it with `VITE_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000` and `VITE_API_KEY=dev-key` in `app/.env.local`.

| Variable | Default | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `PIANOVERSE_API_KEY` | none | Bearer key for every `/api` route except `/api/health`. Without it auth is off, which is allowed only on `127.0.0.1`. |
| `PIANOVERSE_HOST` / `PIANOVERSE_PORT` | `127.0.0.1` / `8000` | Where uvicorn listens. Any other host requires an API key. |
| `PIANOVERSE_DATA_DIR` | `engine/data` | Local database and blobs. |
| `PIANOVERSE_DATABASE_URL` | `sqlite:///<data dir>/pianoverse.db` | Any SQLAlchemy URL (`postgresql+psycopg://…` in the cloud). |
| `PIANOVERSE_CORS_ORIGINS` | `http://localhost:5173` | Comma-separated origins allowed to call the API. |
| `PIANOVERSE_RUNNER` | `local` | `local` runs jobs one at a time on a thread in the server. |
| `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` | none | Cloud storage (later). |

Uploads must be at most 50 MB and 10 seconds to 8 minutes long. Jobs that were running when the server stopped are marked `interrupted` on the next start, and so is any job without progress for 20 minutes. Errors come back as `{"error": {"code", "message"}}`; the app translates by `code`.
````

### Task 5: App — API client, polling, loader source and library from the API

Spec §6.1, §6.2.1, §6.3 (last row). Without `VITE_API_URL` nothing visible changes. With it, the library reads songs from the server, shows running and failed jobs as cards, shows a "server down" note with retry, and the "+ New" button links to `/new`. The `/new` and `/processing/:jobId` routes are added by Tasks 6 and 8 (until then those links fall through to the `*` redirect to `/`), so no placeholder screen ever ships.

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/src/i18n/messages.ts` (whole file below), `app/src/library/LibraryScreen.tsx` (whole file below)
- Modify: `app/src/styles/theme.ts`, `app/src/styles/theme.test.ts`, `app/src/styles/app.css` (append)
- Modify: `app/src/song/loader.ts`, `app/src/song/loader.test.ts`, `app/src/library/LibraryScreen.test.tsx`
- Modify: `app/src/vite-env.d.ts`, `app/vite.config.ts`, `app/.gitignore`
- Create: `app/.env.example`
- Create: `app/src/api/types.ts`, `app/src/api/client.ts`, `app/src/api/client.test.ts`
- Create: `app/src/api/errorText.ts`, `app/src/api/errorText.test.ts`
- Create: `app/src/api/usePoll.ts`, `app/src/api/usePoll.test.ts`
- Create: `app/src/library/JobCards.tsx`
- Create: `app/src/test/apiFetch.ts` (test helper, not a test file)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the API JSON shapes of spec §4 (Tasks 1–4): `GET /api/songs` → `SongSummary[]`, `GET /api/songs/{id}` → Song JSON, `GET /api/jobs?active=1` → `Job[]`, `GET /api/jobs/{id}` → `Job`, `POST /api/jobs` → `202 {"jobId"}`, `DELETE /api/jobs/{id}` → 204, errors `{"error": {"code", "message"}}`, auth `Authorization: Bearer <key>`.
- Produces (`src/api/types.ts`): `JobStatus`, `ActiveJobStatus = Exclude<JobStatus, 'done' | 'failed'>`, `JobSource = 'recording' | 'file'`, `Mode = 'auto' | SourceType`, `MODES: Mode[]`, `ApiErrorCode = string`, `JobPreview`, `Job`, `CreateJobInput = { audio: Blob; filename: string; title: string; mode: Mode; source: JobSource }`.
- Produces (`src/api/client.ts`): `ApiConfig = { url: string; key: string }`, `apiConfig(env = import.meta.env): ApiConfig | null`, `class ApiError extends Error { readonly code: string; readonly status: number; readonly serverMessage: string | undefined }` (status 0 + code `network` when fetch throws; code `bad_response` when the body is not the expected JSON), `FetchLike = (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>`, `interface Api { listSongs(): Promise<SongSummary[]>; getSong(id: string): Promise<unknown>; listJobs(): Promise<Job[]>; getJob(id: string): Promise<Job>; createJob(input: CreateJobInput): Promise<string>; dismissJob(id: string): Promise<void> }`, `createApi(config: ApiConfig, fetchImpl?: FetchLike): Api`, `getApi(fetchImpl?: FetchLike): Api | null`.
- Produces (`src/api/errorText.ts`): `errorText(m: Messages, code: string, message?: string): string` — translation by code, else the server's message, else `m.apiErrors.fallback`.
- Produces (`src/api/usePoll.ts`): `usePoll<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, intervalMs: number, opts?: { enabled?: boolean; stop?: (v: T) => boolean }): { data: T | undefined; error: unknown; refresh: () => void }` — calls `fn` at once, then `intervalMs` after each settle; keeps polling after errors; stops when `stop(value)` is true; `refresh()` polls again immediately.
- Produces (`src/i18n/messages.ts`): `common.close`, `common.langLabels`, `library.newSong`, `library.processingCard` (keyed by `ActiveJobStatus`), `library.dismiss`, `library.serverDown`, `apiErrors` (codes + `fallback`), and the module-level stage-name constants `elStages` / `enStages` that Task 8 reuses for its step labels.
- Produces (theme): CSS variables `--success`, `--text-soft`, `--track`, `--idle`.
- Produces (`src/test/apiFetch.ts`): `API_URL = 'http://api.test'`, `jsonResponse(body, status?)`, `apiFetch(routes: Record<string, (init?: RequestInit) => Response>)` — a `vi.fn` fetch routed by `"METHOD /path?query"`; unrouted calls reject like a dead network.

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { ApiError, apiConfig, createApi, getApi, type FetchLike } from './client'

const config = { url: 'http://api.test', key: 'k1' }
const respond = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  vi.fn<FetchLike>(async () => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status }))
const audio = new Blob(['RIFF'], { type: 'audio/wav' })
const input = { audio, filename: 'take.wav', title: 'Take', mode: 'instrumental' as const, source: 'file' as const }

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllEnvs())

describe('apiConfig', () => {
  it('is off without a URL and trims a trailing slash', () => {
    expect(apiConfig({})).toBeNull()
    expect(apiConfig({ VITE_API_URL: '   ' })).toBeNull()
    expect(apiConfig({ VITE_API_URL: 'http://api.test/', VITE_API_KEY: 'k1' })).toEqual(config)
    expect(apiConfig({ VITE_API_URL: 'http://api.test' })).toEqual({ url: 'http://api.test', key: '' })
  })

  it('reads the Vite env by default', () => {
    expect(apiConfig()).toBeNull()
    expect(getApi()).toBeNull()
    vi.stubEnv('VITE_API_URL', 'http://api.test')
    expect(apiConfig()).toEqual({ url: 'http://api.test', key: '' })
    expect(getApi()).not.toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('createApi', () => {
  it('sends the key and reads songs, jobs and a song', async () => {
    const fetchImpl = respond([{ id: 's1' }])
    const api = createApi(config, fetchImpl)
    await expect(api.listSongs()).resolves.toEqual([{ id: 's1' }])
    await api.listJobs()
    await api.getJob('a b')
    await api.getSong('s1')
    expect(fetchImpl.mock.calls.map(([url]) => url)).toEqual([
      'http://api.test/api/songs',
      'http://api.test/api/jobs?active=1',
      'http://api.test/api/jobs/a%20b',
      'http://api.test/api/songs/s1',
    ])
    expect(fetchImpl.mock.calls[0][1]?.headers).toEqual({ Authorization: 'Bearer k1' })
  })

  it('sends no Authorization header without a key', async () => {
    const fetchImpl = respond([])
    await createApi({ url: 'http://api.test', key: '' }, fetchImpl).listSongs()
    expect(fetchImpl.mock.calls[0][1]?.headers).toEqual({})
  })

  it('posts the audio as multipart and returns the job id', async () => {
    const fetchImpl = respond({ jobId: 'j1' }, 202)
    await expect(createApi(config, fetchImpl).createJob(input)).resolves.toBe('j1')
    const [url, init] = fetchImpl.mock.calls[0]
    expect(url).toBe('http://api.test/api/jobs')
    expect(init?.method).toBe('POST')
    const body = init?.body as FormData
    expect(body.get('title')).toBe('Take')
    expect(body.get('mode')).toBe('instrumental')
    expect(body.get('source')).toBe('file')
    expect((body.get('audio') as File).name).toBe('take.wav')
  })

  it('dismisses a job with DELETE', async () => {
    const fetchImpl = vi.fn<FetchLike>(async () => new Response(null, { status: 204 }))
    await createApi(config, fetchImpl).dismissJob('j2')
    expect(fetchImpl).toHaveBeenCalledWith('http://api.test/api/jobs/j2', expect.objectContaining({ method: 'DELETE' }))
  })

  it('turns error bodies, bare statuses, dead networks and garbage into ApiError', async () => {
    const rejected = createApi(config, respond({ error: { code: 'too_short', message: 'Too short.' } }, 400))
    await expect(rejected.createJob(input)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'too_short', status: 400, serverMessage: 'Too short.' })

    const missing = createApi(config, vi.fn<FetchLike>(async () => new Response('nope', { status: 404 })))
    await expect(missing.getJob('x')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'not_found', status: 404 })

    const down = createApi(config, vi.fn<FetchLike>(async () => { throw new TypeError('offline') }))
    const error = await down.listSongs().catch((e: unknown) => e)
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError)
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ code: 'network', status: 0 })

    const garbled = createApi(config, vi.fn<FetchLike>(async () => new Response('<html>', { status: 200 })))
    await expect(garbled.listSongs()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'bad_response' })

    const noId = createApi(config, respond({}, 202))
    await expect(noId.createJob(input)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'bad_response' })
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the API types and client**

Create `app/src/api/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { Key, SourceType } from '../song/types'

export type JobStatus = 'queued' | 'separating' | 'analyzing' | 'melody' | 'arranging' | 'done' | 'failed'
export type ActiveJobStatus = Exclude<JobStatus, 'done' | 'failed'>
export type JobSource = 'recording' | 'file'
export type Mode = 'auto' | SourceType
export type ApiErrorCode = string

export const MODES: Mode[] = ['auto', 'vocal', 'instrumental', 'solo_piano']

export interface JobPreview {
  key: Key
  bpm: number
  timeSignature: [number, number]
  chords: string[]
}

export interface Job {
  id: string
  title: string
  status: JobStatus
  progress: number
  source: JobSource
  durationSec: number
  createdAt: string
  songId?: string
  preview?: JobPreview
  error?: { code: ApiErrorCode; message: string }
}

export interface CreateJobInput {
  audio: Blob
  filename: string
  title: string
  mode: Mode
  source: JobSource
}
```

Create `app/src/api/client.ts`:

```ts
import type { SongSummary } from '../song/types'
import type { CreateJobInput, Job } from './types'

export interface ApiConfig {
  url: string
  key: string
}

const text = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim() : '')

export function apiConfig(env: Record<string, unknown> = import.meta.env): ApiConfig | null {
  const url = text(env.VITE_API_URL).replace(/\/+$/, '')
  return url ? { url, key: text(env.VITE_API_KEY) } : null
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly code: string
  readonly status: number
  readonly serverMessage: string | undefined

  constructor(code: string, status: number, serverMessage?: string) {
    super(serverMessage ?? code)
    this.name = 'ApiError'
    this.code = code
    this.status = status
    this.serverMessage = serverMessage
  }
}

export type FetchLike = (input: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>

export interface Api {
  listSongs(): Promise<SongSummary[]>
  getSong(id: string): Promise<unknown>
  listJobs(): Promise<Job[]>
  getJob(id: string): Promise<Job>
  createJob(input: CreateJobInput): Promise<string>
  dismissJob(id: string): Promise<void>
}

async function toError(res: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
  try {
    const body = (await res.json()) as { error?: { code?: unknown; message?: unknown } }
    const { code, message } = body.error ?? {}
    if (typeof code === 'string') return new ApiError(code, res.status, typeof message === 'string' ? message : undefined)
  } catch {
    // not our JSON error shape: fall back to the status
  }
  return new ApiError(res.status === 404 ? 'not_found' : `http_${res.status}`, res.status)
}

// The default looks up the global fetch per call, so test stubs installed later still apply.
export function createApi(config: ApiConfig, fetchImpl: FetchLike = (input, init) => fetch(input, init)): Api {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = config.key ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${config.key}` } : {}

  async function send(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
    let res: Response
    try {
      res = await fetchImpl(`${config.url}/api${path}`, { ...init, headers })
    } catch {
      throw new ApiError('network', 0)
    }
    if (!res.ok) throw await toError(res)
    return res
  }

  async function json<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    const res = await send(path, init)
    try {
      return (await res.json()) as T
    } catch {
      throw new ApiError('bad_response', res.status)
    }
  }

  const id = encodeURIComponent
  return {
    listSongs: () => json<SongSummary[]>('/songs'),
    getSong: (songId) => json<unknown>(`/songs/${id(songId)}`),
    listJobs: () => json<Job[]>('/jobs?active=1'),
    getJob: (jobId) => json<Job>(`/jobs/${id(jobId)}`),
    async createJob(input) {
      const body = new FormData()
      body.append('audio', input.audio, input.filename)
      body.append('title', input.title)
      body.append('mode', input.mode)
      body.append('source', input.source)
      const res = await json<{ jobId?: unknown }>('/jobs', { method: 'POST', body })
      if (typeof res.jobId !== 'string') throw new ApiError('bad_response', 202)
      return res.jobId
    },
    async dismissJob(jobId) {
      await send(`/jobs/${id(jobId)}`, { method: 'DELETE' })
    },
  }
}

export function getApi(fetchImpl?: FetchLike): Api | null {
  const config = apiConfig()
  return config ? createApi(config, fetchImpl) : null
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Write `usePoll`**

Create `app/src/api/usePoll.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

export interface PollOptions<T> {
  enabled?: boolean
  stop?: (value: T) => boolean
}

export interface Poll<T> {
  data: T | undefined
  error: unknown
  refresh: () => void
}

export function usePoll<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, intervalMs: number, opts: PollOptions<T> = {}): Poll<T> {
  const { enabled = true } = opts
  // Latest callbacks without restarting the loop on every render.
  const fnRef = useRef(fn)
  const stopRef = useRef(opts.stop)
  fnRef.current = fn
  stopRef.current = opts.stop

  const [data, setData] = useState<T>()
  const [error, setError] = useState<unknown>()
  const [round, setRound] = useState(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return
    let alive = true
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
    // setTimeout after each settle (not setInterval) so a slow server never gets overlapping requests.
    const tick = async () => {
      let again = true
      try {
        const value = await fnRef.current()
        if (!alive) return
        setData(value)
        setError(undefined)
        again = !stopRef.current?.(value)
      } catch (e) {
        if (!alive) return
        setError(e)
      }
      if (again) timer = setTimeout(tick, intervalMs)
    }
    void tick()
    return () => {
      alive = false
      clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [enabled, intervalMs, round])

  const refresh = useCallback(() => setRound((r) => r + 1), [])
  return { data, error, refresh }
}
```

Create `app/src/test/apiFetch.ts`:

```ts
import { vi } from 'vitest'

export const API_URL = 'http://api.test'

type Handler = (init?: RequestInit) => Response

export const jsonResponse = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status })

// A fetch stand-in routed by "METHOD /path?query"; unrouted calls reject the way a dead network does.
export function apiFetch(routes: Record<string, Handler>) {
  return vi.fn(async (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const key = `${init?.method ?? 'GET'} ${url.replace(API_URL, '')}`
    const handler = routes[key]
    if (!handler) throw new TypeError(`no route for ${key}`)
    return handler(init)
  })
}
```

In `app/src/song/loader.ts`, add the import at the top:

```ts
import { ApiError, getApi } from '../api/client'
```

and replace `loadSong` and `loadIndex` (lines 50–58) with:

```ts
async function fromApi<T>(call: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  try {
    return await call()
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'not_found') throw new SongLoadError('not_found')
    if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'bad_response') throw new SongLoadError('schema_mismatch')
    throw new SongLoadError('network')
  }
}

export async function loadSong(id: string, fetchImpl: FetchLike = (u) => fetch(u)): Promise<Song> {
  const api = getApi()
  return parseSong(api ? await fromApi(() => api.getSong(id)) : await getJson(songUrl(id), fetchImpl))
}

export async function loadIndex(fetchImpl: FetchLike = (u) => fetch(u)): Promise<SongSummary[]> {
  const api = getApi()
  const data: unknown = api ? await fromApi(() => api.listSongs()) : await getJson('/songs/index.json', fetchImpl)
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) throw new SongLoadError('schema_mismatch')
  return data as SongSummary[]
}
```

```tsx
        if (!up) throw new TypeError('down')
        return jsonResponse(index)
      },
      'GET /api/jobs?active=1': () => jsonResponse([]),
    }))
    const user = userEvent.setup()
    render(<MemoryRouter><LibraryScreen /></MemoryRouter>)
    expect(await screen.findByText('Δεν βρίσκω τον server.')).toBeInTheDocument()
    up = true
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Δοκίμασε ξανά' }))
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: /Demo · Am F C G/ })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByText('Δεν βρίσκω τον server.')).not.toBeInTheDocument()
  })
})
```
