- [ ] **Step 15: Build the job cards and the API-aware library**

Create `app/src/library/JobCards.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { errorText } from '../api/errorText'
import type { ActiveJobStatus, Job } from '../api/types'
import { useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { Alert } from '../ui/icons'

export function JobCard({ job }: { job: Job }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const stage: ActiveJobStatus = job.status === 'done' || job.status === 'failed' ? 'arranging' : job.status
  const width = `${Math.round(Math.min(1, Math.max(0.04, job.progress)) * 100)}%`
  return (
    <Link to={`/processing/${encodeURIComponent(job.id)}`} className="song-card job-card">
      <span className="job-thumb" aria-hidden="true"><span /><span /><span /></span>
      <span className="song-body">
        <span className="song-title">{job.title}</span>
        <span className="song-meta">{m.library.processingCard[stage]}</span>
        <span className="job-bar" aria-hidden="true"><span style={{ width }} /></span>
      </span>
    </Link>
  )
}

export function FailedJobCard({ job, onDismiss }: { job: Job; onDismiss: () => void }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  return (
    <div className="song-card job-card">
      <span className="job-thumb failed" aria-hidden="true"><Alert /></span>
      <span className="song-body">
        <span className="song-title">{job.title}</span>
        <span className="job-error">{errorText(m, job.error?.code ?? 'internal', job.error?.message)}</span>
      </span>
      <button type="button" className="text-btn" onClick={onDismiss}>{m.library.dismiss}</button>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Replace `app/src/library/LibraryScreen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { getApi } from '../api/client'
import type { Job } from '../api/types'
import { usePoll } from '../api/usePoll'
import { useLang, useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { keyName } from '../i18n/music'
import { SongLoadError, loadIndex, type SongLoadErrorCode } from '../song/loader'
import type { SongSummary } from '../song/types'
import { formatTime } from '../player/timeline'
import { Alert, Plus } from '../ui/icons'
import { FailedJobCard, JobCard } from './JobCards'

export const JOBS_POLL_MS = 2000

function Thumb({ seed }: { seed: string }) {
  let h = 7
  for (const ch of seed) h = (h * 31 + ch.charCodeAt(0)) % 997
  return (
    <span className="thumb-roll" aria-hidden="true">
      {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => {
        const height = 10 + ((h * (i + 3)) % 22)
        const top = 6 + ((h * (i + 1) * 5) % (44 - height))
        return <span key={i} className={i % 2 === 0 ? 'bar left' : 'bar right'} style={{ left: 4 + i * 10, top, height }} />
      })}
    </span>
  )
}

function SongCard({ song }: { song: SongSummary }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const lang = useLang((s) => s.lang)
  return (
    <Link to={`/song/${encodeURIComponent(song.id)}`} className="song-card">
      <Thumb seed={song.id} />
      <span className="song-body">
        <span className="song-title">{song.title}</span>
        <span className="song-meta">{`${keyName(song.key.tonic, song.key.mode, lang)} · ${Math.round(song.bpm)} BPM · ${formatTime(song.durationSec)}`}</span>
        <span className="song-chords">
          {song.chords.slice(0, 4).map((c, i) => <span key={`${c}-${i}`} className="chip">{c}</span>)}
        </span>
      </span>
      <span className="song-tag">{m.library.sourceType[song.sourceType]}</span>
    </Link>
  )
}

export function LibraryScreen() {
  const m = useMessages()
  const lang = useLang((s) => s.lang)
  const setLang = useLang((s) => s.setLang)
  const api = useMemo(() => getApi(), [])
  const jobs = usePoll(() => (api ? api.listJobs() : Promise.resolve<Job[]>([])), JOBS_POLL_MS, { enabled: api !== null })
  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState<string[]>([])
  const [reload, setReload] = useState(0)
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ songs?: SongSummary[]; error?: SongLoadErrorCode }>({})

  const visible = (jobs.data ?? []).filter((job) => !dismissed.includes(job.id))
  const active = visible.filter((job) => job.status !== 'failed' && job.status !== 'done')
  const failed = visible.filter((job) => job.status === 'failed')
  const activeKey = active.map((job) => job.id).join(',')

  // Reloads whenever the set of running jobs changes, so a finished job turns into a song card.
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    loadIndex().then(
      (songs) => alive && setState({ songs }),
      (e: unknown) => alive && setState({ error: e instanceof SongLoadError ? e.code : 'network' }),
    )
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [activeKey, reload])

  const retry = () => {
    setReload((n) => n + 1)
    jobs.refresh()
  }
  const dismiss = (id: string) => {
    setDismissed((ids) => [...ids, id])
    // Hidden at once; if the DELETE fails the card simply comes back on the next visit.
    void api?.dismissJob(id).catch(() => undefined)
  }
  const serverDown = api !== null && state.error === 'network'

  return (
    <div className="library">
      <header className="lib-header">
        <span className="logo" aria-hidden="true"><span className="l1" /><span className="l2" /><span className="l3" /></span>
        <h1 className="wordmark">Pianoverse</h1>
        <div className="segmented small" role="group" aria-label={m.library.language}>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={lang === 'el'} onClick={() => setLang('el')}>{m.common.langLabels.el}</button>
          <button type="button" aria-pressed={lang === 'en'} onClick={() => setLang('en')}>{m.common.langLabels.en}</button>
        </div>
      </header>
      <h2 className="section-label">{m.library.yourSongs}</h2>
      {serverDown && (
        <div className="server-down" role="alert">
          <Alert />
          <span>{m.library.serverDown}</span>
          <button type="button" className="text-btn" onClick={retry}>{m.common.retry}</button>
        </div>
      )}
      {state.error && !serverDown && <p className="lib-note">{m.errors[state.error]}</p>}
      {state.songs?.length === 0 && visible.length === 0 && <p className="lib-note">{m.library.empty}</p>}
      <ul className="song-list">
        {active.map((job) => <li key={job.id}><JobCard job={job} /></li>)}
        {failed.map((job) => <li key={job.id}><FailedJobCard job={job} onDismiss={() => dismiss(job.id)} /></li>)}
        {state.songs?.map((song) => <li key={song.id}><SongCard song={song} /></li>)}
      </ul>
      {api ? (
        <Link to="/new" className="new-song-btn"><Plus />{m.library.newSong}</Link>
      ) : (
        <div className="new-song-soon"><Plus />{m.library.newSongSoon}</div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `app/src/newsong/title.ts`:

```ts
import { messages, type Lang } from '../i18n/messages'

export type TitleSource = { kind: 'file'; name: string } | { kind: 'recording'; at: Date }

const LOCALE: Record<Lang, string> = { el: 'el-GR', en: 'en-US' }

export function defaultTitle(source: TitleSource, lang: Lang): string {
  if (source.kind === 'file') return source.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '').trim() || source.name
  const locale = LOCALE[lang]
  const day = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }).format(source.at)
  const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale, { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23' }).format(source.at)
  return `${messages[lang].newSong.recordingTitle} ${day}, ${time}`
}
```

```tsx
})

it('speaks English too', () => {
```

Create `app/src/newsong/probe.ts`:

```ts
// The browser's own decoder gives the length for display; the server re-checks it with ffprobe.
export function probeDuration(file: Blob, timeoutMs = 5000): Promise<number | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file)
    const audio = new Audio()
    const finish = (value: number | null) => {
      clearTimeout(timer)
      audio.onloadedmetadata = null
      audio.onerror = null
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
      resolve(value)
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => finish(null), timeoutMs)
    audio.preload = 'metadata'
    audio.onloadedmetadata = () => finish(Number.isFinite(audio.duration) ? audio.duration : null)
    audio.onerror = () => finish(null)
    audio.src = url
  })
}
```

```ts
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { ApiError, type Api } from '../api/client'
import type { CreateJobInput } from '../api/types'

export type SubmitState =
  | { status: 'idle' }
  | { status: 'sending' }
  | { status: 'error'; code: string; message?: string }

export function useSubmit(api: Api) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [state, setState] = useState<SubmitState>({ status: 'idle' })

  const send = async (input: CreateJobInput) => {
    setState({ status: 'sending' })
    try {
      const jobId = await api.createJob(input)
      navigate(`/processing/${encodeURIComponent(jobId)}`)
    } catch (e) {
      setState(e instanceof ApiError ? { status: 'error', code: e.code, message: e.serverMessage } : { status: 'error', code: 'internal' })
    }
  }
  const clear = () => setState({ status: 'idle' })

  return { state, send, clear }
}
```

```tsx
import { useId } from 'react'
import { useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { formatTime } from '../player/timeline'
import { AudioFile } from '../ui/icons'

export interface PickedFile {
  file: File
  durationSec: number | null
}

export function FilePanel({ picked, onPick }: { picked: PickedFile | null; onPick: (file: File) => void }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const inputId = useId()
  return (
    <div className="file-panel">
      <input
        id={inputId}
        className="sr-only"
        type="file"
        accept="audio/*"
        onChange={(e) => {
          const file = e.target.files?.[0]
          if (file) onPick(file)
          // Lets the same file be picked again after an error.
          e.target.value = ''
        }}
      />
      <label htmlFor={inputId} className="file-pick">
        <span className="file-icon"><AudioFile /></span>
        {picked ? (
          <span className="file-body">
            <span className="file-name">{picked.file.name}</span>
            <span className="file-meta">{picked.durationSec !== null && `${formatTime(picked.durationSec)} · `}{m.newSong.changeFile}</span>
          </span>
        ) : (
          <span className="file-body">
            <span className="file-name">{m.newSong.pickFile}</span>
            <span className="file-meta">{m.newSong.pickHint}</span>
          </span>
        )}
      </label>
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useId } from 'react'
import { MODES, type Mode } from '../api/types'
import { useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'

export function ModeChips({ value, onChange }: { value: Mode; onChange: (mode: Mode) => void }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const labelId = useId()
  return (
    <section className="field">
      <div className="field-label" id={labelId}>{m.newSong.whatIsIt}</div>
      <div className="mode-chips" role="group" aria-labelledby={labelId}>
        {MODES.map((mode) => (
          <button key={mode} type="button" className="mode-chip" aria-pressed={value === mode} onClick={() => onChange(mode)}>
            {m.newSong.modes[mode]}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

Create `app/src/newsong/NewSongScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, Navigate } from 'react-router'
import { getApi, type Api } from '../api/client'
import { errorText } from '../api/errorText'
import type { Mode } from '../api/types'
import { useLang, useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { Close } from '../ui/icons'
import { FilePanel, type PickedFile } from './FilePanel'
import { ModeChips } from './ModeChips'
import { probeDuration } from './probe'
import { defaultTitle } from './title'
import { useSubmit } from './useSubmit'

export interface NewSongProps {
  api: Api
  probe?: (file: Blob) => Promise<number | null>
}

export function NewSong({ api, probe = probeDuration }: NewSongProps) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const lang = useLang((s) => s.lang)
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('auto')
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<PickedFile | null>(null)
  const submit = useSubmit(api)

  const pick = (file: File) => {
    submit.clear()
    setPicked({ file, durationSec: null })
    void probe(file).then((durationSec) => setPicked((p) => (p?.file === file ? { file, durationSec } : p)))
  }
  const send = () => {
    if (!picked) return
    const { file } = picked
    void submit.send({ audio: file, filename: file.name, title: defaultTitle({ kind: 'file', name: file.name }, lang), mode, source: 'file' })
  }
  const sending = submit.state.status === 'sending'
  const error = submit.state.status === 'error' ? submit.state : null

  return (
    <div className="screen new-song">
      <header className="screen-head">
        <Link to="/" className="icon-btn" aria-label={m.common.close}><Close /></Link>
        <h1 className="screen-title">{m.newSong.title}</h1>
      </header>
      <FilePanel picked={picked} onPick={pick} />
      <ModeChips value={mode} onChange={setMode} />
      {error && (
        <div className="form-error" role="alert">
          <span>{errorText(m, error.code, error.message)}</span>
          {error.code === 'network' && <button type="button" className="text-btn" onClick={send}>{m.common.retry}</button>}
        </div>
      )}
      <button type="button" className="cta" disabled={!picked || sending} onClick={send}>
        {sending ? m.newSong.sending : m.newSong.submit}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

export function NewSongScreen() {
  const api = useMemo(() => getApi(), [])
  return api ? <NewSong api={api} /> : <Navigate to="/" replace />
}
```

```tsx
import { BrowserRouter, Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { LibraryScreen } from './library/LibraryScreen'
import { NewSongScreen } from './newsong/NewSongScreen'
import { PlayerScreen } from './player/PlayerScreen'

export function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<LibraryScreen />} />
        <Route path="/new" element={<NewSongScreen />} />
        <Route path="/song/:id" element={<PlayerScreen />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
      </Routes>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}
```

### Task 7: App — "New song": record from the microphone

Spec §6.2.2 (recording), §6.3 (rows 1–3). Reproduces the `Record` mockup: segmented "Record | From a file", big JetBrains Mono timer with "Listening… · up to 8:00", live waveform bars, the 104 px orange button with glow inside a 168 px ring, mode chips, the tip card, and the white "Done — turn it into piano" button. Deviations from the mockup per the spec: a start state (dark dot glyph, "Tap to start") before recording; "Done" while recording stops and sends in one tap; under 10 s a warning shows and "Done" stays disabled; a card replaces the recorder when the mic is blocked, missing, the origin is not secure, or the browser can't record.

All browser media APIs sit behind injected dependencies (`RecorderDeps` for `createRecorder`, `MakeRecorder` for the hook and the screen); jsdom has none of them, so tests use fakes.

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import {
  MAX_RECORDING_SEC,
  RecorderError,
  createRecorder,
  extensionFor,
  pickMimeType,
  rms,
  type MediaRecorderLike,
  type RecorderDeps,
} from './recorder'

function fakes(overrides: Partial<RecorderDeps> = {}) {
  const track = { stop: vi.fn() }
  const stream = { getTracks: () => [track] }
  let push: (chunk: Blob) => void = () => {}
  const media: MediaRecorderLike = {
    mimeType: 'audio/webm;codecs=opus',
    start: vi.fn((_timesliceMs: number, onData: (chunk: Blob) => void) => {
      push = onData
    }),
    stop: vi.fn(async () => push(new Blob(['tail']))),
  }
  const meter = { level: () => 0.25, close: vi.fn() }
  let clock = 1_000_000
  const deps: RecorderDeps = {
    isSecureContext: true,
    getUserMedia: vi.fn(async () => stream),
    createMediaRecorder: vi.fn(() => media),
    isTypeSupported: (t) => t === 'audio/webm;codecs=opus',
    createLevelMeter: () => meter,
    now: () => clock,
    ...overrides,
  }
  return {
    deps,
    track,
    media,
    meter,
    push: (chunk: Blob) => push(chunk),
    advance: (ms: number) => {
      clock += ms
    },
  }
}

afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers())

describe('createRecorder', () => {
  it('records, times, meters and returns the audio with a matching file name', async () => {
    const f = fakes()
    const rec = createRecorder(f.deps)
    await rec.start()
    expect(f.deps.createMediaRecorder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.anything(), 'audio/webm;codecs=opus')
    f.push(new Blob(['abc']))
    f.advance(27_000)
    expect(rec.elapsedSec()).toBe(27)
    expect(rec.level()).toBe(0.25)

    const out = await rec.stop()
    expect(out.filename).toBe('recording.webm')
    expect(out.mimeType).toBe('audio/webm;codecs=opus')
    expect(out.durationSec).toBe(27)
    expect(out.startedAt.getTime()).toBe(1_000_000)
    expect(out.blob.size).toBe('abc'.length + 'tail'.length)
    expect(f.track.stop).toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(f.meter.close).toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('maps an insecure origin, missing APIs, a blocked or missing mic to codes', async () => {
    const codeOf = (deps: RecorderDeps) =>
      createRecorder(deps).start().then(
        () => 'ok',
        (e: unknown) => (e instanceof RecorderError ? e.code : 'other'),
      )
    expect(await codeOf(fakes({ isSecureContext: false }).deps)).toBe('insecure')
    expect(await codeOf(fakes({ getUserMedia: null }).deps)).toBe('unsupported')
    expect(await codeOf(fakes({ createMediaRecorder: null }).deps)).toBe('unsupported')
    expect(await codeOf(fakes({ getUserMedia: async () => { throw new DOMException('no', 'NotAllowedError') } }).deps)).toBe('denied')
    expect(await codeOf(fakes({ getUserMedia: async () => { throw new DOMException('no', 'NotFoundError') } }).deps)).toBe('no_mic')
  })

  it('stops by itself at 8:00', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers()
    const f = fakes()
    const onAutoStop = vi.fn()
    const rec = createRecorder(f.deps, { onAutoStop })
    await rec.start()
    f.advance(MAX_RECORDING_SEC * 1000)
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(MAX_RECORDING_SEC * 1000)
    expect(onAutoStop).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ durationSec: 480 }))
    expect(f.track.stop).toHaveBeenCalled()
  })

  it('cancel releases the microphone without a result', async () => {
    const f = fakes()
    const rec = createRecorder(f.deps)
    await rec.start()
    rec.cancel()
    expect(f.media.stop).toHaveBeenCalled()
    expect(f.track.stop).toHaveBeenCalled()
    await expect(rec.stop()).rejects.toThrow('not recording')
  })
})

describe('helpers', () => {
  it('picks the first container the browser supports', () => {
    expect(pickMimeType((t) => t.startsWith('audio/mp4'))).toBe('audio/mp4;codecs=mp4a.40.2')
    expect(pickMimeType(() => false)).toBeUndefined()
  })

  it('names files after the container', () => {
    expect(extensionFor('audio/webm;codecs=opus')).toBe('.webm')
    expect(extensionFor('audio/mp4')).toBe('.m4a')
    expect(extensionFor('audio/ogg;codecs=opus')).toBe('.ogg')
    expect(extensionFor('audio/x-unknown')).toBe('.bin')
  })

  it('measures loudness as RMS of the centred samples', () => {
    expect(rms(new Uint8Array([128, 128]))).toBe(0)
    expect(rms(new Uint8Array([0, 255]))).toBeCloseTo(0.996, 2)
    expect(rms(new Uint8Array([]))).toBe(0)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the recorder**

Create `app/src/newsong/recorder.ts`:

```ts
export const MAX_RECORDING_SEC = 8 * 60
export const MIN_RECORDING_SEC = 10

export type RecorderErrorCode = 'denied' | 'no_mic' | 'insecure' | 'unsupported'

export class RecorderError extends Error {
  readonly code: RecorderErrorCode

  constructor(code: RecorderErrorCode) {
    super(code)
    this.name = 'RecorderError'
    this.code = code
  }
}

export interface StreamLike {
  getTracks(): { stop(): void }[]
}

export interface MediaRecorderLike {
  readonly mimeType: string
  start(timesliceMs: number, onData: (chunk: Blob) => void): void
  /** Resolves after the last chunk has been delivered. */
  stop(): Promise<void>
}

export interface LevelMeter {
  level(): number
  close(): void
}

export interface RecorderDeps {
  isSecureContext: boolean
  getUserMedia: ((constraints: { audio: true }) => Promise<StreamLike>) | null
  createMediaRecorder: ((stream: StreamLike, mimeType: string | undefined) => MediaRecorderLike) | null
  isTypeSupported: (mimeType: string) => boolean
  createLevelMeter: (stream: StreamLike) => LevelMeter | null
  now: () => number
}

export interface Recording {
  blob: Blob
  mimeType: string
  filename: string
  durationSec: number
  startedAt: Date
}

export interface Recorder {
  start(): Promise<void>
  stop(): Promise<Recording>
  elapsedSec(): number
  level(): number
  cancel(): void
}

// Chrome/Firefox record webm/opus, Safari only mp4/aac; the server's ffprobe reads all of them.
const MIME_CANDIDATES = ['audio/webm;codecs=opus', 'audio/webm', 'audio/mp4;codecs=mp4a.40.2', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/ogg;codecs=opus']

export function pickMimeType(isTypeSupported: (mimeType: string) => boolean): string | undefined {
  return MIME_CANDIDATES.find((t) => isTypeSupported(t))
}

export function extensionFor(mimeType: string): string {
  if (mimeType.startsWith('audio/webm')) return '.webm'
  if (mimeType.startsWith('audio/mp4')) return '.m4a'
  if (mimeType.startsWith('audio/ogg')) return '.ogg'
  return '.bin'
}

export function rms(samples: Uint8Array): number {
  if (samples.length === 0) return 0
  let sum = 0
  for (const v of samples) {
    const x = (v - 128) / 128
    sum += x * x
  }
  return Math.sqrt(sum / samples.length)
}

function mediaError(e: unknown): RecorderError {
  const name = typeof e === 'object' && e !== null && 'name' in e ? String(e.name) : ''
  return new RecorderError(name === 'NotAllowedError' || name === 'SecurityError' ? 'denied' : 'no_mic')
}

function wrapMediaRecorder(media: MediaRecorder): MediaRecorderLike {
  return {
    get mimeType() {
      return media.mimeType
    },
    start(timesliceMs, onData) {
      media.ondataavailable = (e) => {
        if (e.data.size > 0) onData(e.data)
      }
      media.start(timesliceMs)
    },
    stop() {
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        if (media.state === 'inactive') return resolve()
        media.onstop = () => resolve()
        media.stop()
      })
    },
  }
}

function browserLevelMeter(stream: StreamLike): LevelMeter | null {
  if (typeof AudioContext === 'undefined') return null
  const ctx = new AudioContext()
  const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser()
  analyser.fftSize = 1024
  ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream as MediaStream).connect(analyser)
  const samples = new Uint8Array(analyser.fftSize)
  return {
    level() {
      analyser.getByteTimeDomainData(samples)
      return rms(samples)
    },
    close() {
      void ctx.close()
    },
  }
}

export function browserRecorderDeps(): RecorderDeps {
  // mediaDevices is undefined on insecure origins even though the DOM types say otherwise.
  const devices = (typeof navigator === 'undefined' ? undefined : navigator.mediaDevices) as MediaDevices | undefined
  const Media = typeof MediaRecorder === 'undefined' ? null : MediaRecorder
  return {
    isSecureContext: globalThis.isSecureContext ?? false,
    getUserMedia: devices ? (constraints) => devices.getUserMedia(constraints) : null,
    createMediaRecorder: Media
      ? (stream, mimeType) => wrapMediaRecorder(new Media(stream as MediaStream, mimeType ? { mimeType } : undefined))
      : null,
    isTypeSupported: (mimeType) => Media?.isTypeSupported(mimeType) ?? false,
    createLevelMeter: browserLevelMeter,
    now: () => Date.now(),
  }
}

export function createRecorder(
  deps: RecorderDeps = browserRecorderDeps(),
  opts: { onAutoStop?: (recording: Recording) => void } = {},
): Recorder {
  let stream: StreamLike | null = null
  let media: MediaRecorderLike | null = null
  let meter: LevelMeter | null = null
  let chunks: Blob[] = []
  let startedAt: number | null = null
  let stoppedAt: number | null = null
  let autoStop: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined

  const release = () => {
    clearTimeout(autoStop)
    meter?.close()
    meter = null
    stream?.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop())
    stream = null
    media = null
  }

  async function start(): Promise<void> {
    if (!deps.isSecureContext) throw new RecorderError('insecure')
    if (!deps.getUserMedia || !deps.createMediaRecorder) throw new RecorderError('unsupported')
    try {
      stream = await deps.getUserMedia({ audio: true })
    } catch (e) {
      throw mediaError(e)
    }
    try {
      media = deps.createMediaRecorder(stream, pickMimeType(deps.isTypeSupported))
    } catch {
      release()
      throw new RecorderError('unsupported')
    }
    chunks = []
    meter = deps.createLevelMeter(stream)
    media.start(1000, (chunk) => chunks.push(chunk))
    startedAt = deps.now()
    stoppedAt = null
    autoStop = setTimeout(() => {
      stop().then((recording) => opts.onAutoStop?.(recording), () => undefined)
    }, MAX_RECORDING_SEC * 1000)
  }

  async function stop(): Promise<Recording> {
    const current = media
    const begin = startedAt
    if (!current || begin === null) throw new Error('not recording')
    media = null
    clearTimeout(autoStop)
    const end = deps.now()
    stoppedAt = end
    await current.stop()
    release()
    const mimeType = current.mimeType || 'audio/webm'
    return {
      blob: new Blob(chunks, { type: mimeType }),
      mimeType,
      filename: `recording${extensionFor(mimeType)}`,
      durationSec: (end - begin) / 1000,
      startedAt: new Date(begin),
    }
  }

  return {
    start,
    stop,
    elapsedSec: () => (startedAt === null ? 0 : ((stoppedAt ?? deps.now()) - startedAt) / 1000),
    level: () => meter?.level() ?? 0,
    cancel() {
      const current = media
      release()
      void current?.stop().catch(() => undefined)
    },
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the hook**

Create `app/src/newsong/useRecorder.ts`:

```ts
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { RecorderError, createRecorder, type Recorder, type RecorderErrorCode, type Recording } from './recorder'

export const WAVE_BARS = 44
const TICK_MS = 100
// Speech or music near a phone mic sits around 0.05–0.25 RMS; this fills the bar range.
const LEVEL_GAIN = 4

export type RecorderState =
  | { phase: 'idle' }
  | { phase: 'starting' }
  | { phase: 'recording'; elapsedSec: number; levels: number[] }
  | { phase: 'done'; recording: Recording }
  | { phase: 'error'; code: RecorderErrorCode }

export type MakeRecorder = (onAutoStop: (recording: Recording) => void) => Recorder

const browserRecorder: MakeRecorder = (onAutoStop) => createRecorder(undefined, { onAutoStop })

export function useRecorder(make: MakeRecorder = browserRecorder) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<RecorderState>({ phase: 'idle' })
  const recRef = useRef<Recorder | null>(null)
  const lastRef = useRef<Recording | null>(null)

  const finish = useCallback((recording: Recording) => {
    lastRef.current = recording
    setState({ phase: 'done', recording })
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => () => recRef.current?.cancel(), [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (state.phase !== 'recording') return
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      const rec = recRef.current
      if (!rec) return
      const level = Math.min(1, rec.level() * LEVEL_GAIN)
      setState((s) =>
        s.phase === 'recording' ? { phase: 'recording', elapsedSec: rec.elapsedSec(), levels: [...s.levels, level].slice(-WAVE_BARS) } : s,
      )
    }, TICK_MS)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [state.phase])

  const start = useCallback(async () => {
    recRef.current?.cancel()
    lastRef.current = null
    const rec = make(finish)
    recRef.current = rec
    setState({ phase: 'starting' })
    try {
      await rec.start()
      setState({ phase: 'recording', elapsedSec: 0, levels: [] })
    } catch (e) {
      recRef.current = null
      setState({ phase: 'error', code: e instanceof RecorderError ? e.code : 'no_mic' })
    }
  }, [make, finish])

  const stop = useCallback(async (): Promise<Recording | null> => {
    const rec = recRef.current
    if (!rec) return lastRef.current
    try {
      const recording = await rec.stop()
      finish(recording)
      return recording
    } catch {
      // Already stopped by the 8:00 limit; that take is the one to use.
      return lastRef.current
    }
  }, [finish])

  return { state, start, stop }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the record panel and the two-tab screen**

Create `app/src/newsong/RecordPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { formatTime } from '../player/timeline'
import { Alert, MicOff } from '../ui/icons'
import type { RecorderErrorCode } from './recorder'
import { WAVE_BARS, type RecorderState } from './useRecorder'

function Waveform({ levels }: { levels: number[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="wave" aria-hidden="true">
      {Array.from({ length: WAVE_BARS }, (_, i) => {
        const level = levels[i]
        const kind = level === undefined ? 'idle' : i % 3 === 0 ? 'hi' : ''
        return <span key={i} className={`wave-bar ${kind}`} style={{ height: 8 + Math.round((level ?? 0) * 68) }} />
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

export function RecordPanel({ state, onStart, onStop }: { state: RecorderState; onStart: () => void; onStop: () => void }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const recording = state.phase === 'recording'
  const seconds = state.phase === 'recording' ? state.elapsedSec : state.phase === 'done' ? state.recording.durationSec : 0
  const hint = recording ? m.newSong.listening : state.phase === 'done' ? m.newSong.recorded : m.newSong.tapToStart
  return (
    <div className="record-panel">
      <div className="rec-clock">
        <span className="rec-time" role="timer">{formatTime(seconds)}</span>
        <span className="rec-hint">{hint}</span>
      </div>
      <Waveform levels={recording ? state.levels : []} />
      <div className={recording ? 'rec-ring live' : 'rec-ring'}>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="rec-btn"
          aria-label={recording ? m.newSong.stop : m.newSong.start}
          disabled={state.phase === 'starting'}
          onClick={recording ? onStop : onStart}
        >
          <span className={recording ? 'rec-glyph stop' : 'rec-glyph start'} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function MicCard({ code, onRetry, onUpload }: { code: RecorderErrorCode; onRetry: () => void; onUpload: () => void }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const canRetry = code === 'denied' || code === 'no_mic'
  return (
    <div className="mic-card" role="alert">
      <span className="mic-icon"><MicOff /></span>
      <h2 className="mic-title">{m.newSong.mic.title}</h2>
      <p className="mic-text">{m.newSong.mic[code]}</p>
      <div className="mic-actions">
        <button type="button" className="pill-btn primary" onClick={onUpload}>{m.newSong.mic.upload}</button>
        {canRetry && <button type="button" className="pill-btn" onClick={onRetry}>{m.common.retry}</button>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function RecordTip() {
  const m = useMessages()
  return (
    <div className="tip-card">
      <Alert />
      <p>{m.newSong.tip}</p>
    </div>
  )
}
```

Replace `app/src/newsong/NewSongScreen.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, Navigate } from 'react-router'
import { getApi, type Api } from '../api/client'
import { errorText } from '../api/errorText'
import type { Mode } from '../api/types'
import { useLang, useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { Close } from '../ui/icons'
import { FilePanel, type PickedFile } from './FilePanel'
import { ModeChips } from './ModeChips'
import { probeDuration } from './probe'
import { MicCard, RecordPanel, RecordTip } from './RecordPanel'
import { MIN_RECORDING_SEC, type Recording } from './recorder'
import { defaultTitle } from './title'
import { useRecorder, type MakeRecorder } from './useRecorder'
import { useSubmit } from './useSubmit'

type Tab = 'record' | 'file'

export interface NewSongProps {
  api: Api
  probe?: (file: Blob) => Promise<number | null>
  makeRecorder?: MakeRecorder
}

export function NewSong({ api, probe = probeDuration, makeRecorder }: NewSongProps) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const lang = useLang((s) => s.lang)
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('record')
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('auto')
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<PickedFile | null>(null)
  const recorder = useRecorder(makeRecorder)
  const submit = useSubmit(api)
  const rec = recorder.state

  const switchTab = (next: Tab) => {
    if (rec.phase === 'recording') void recorder.stop()
    submit.clear()
    setTab(next)
  }
  const pick = (file: File) => {
    submit.clear()
    setPicked({ file, durationSec: null })
    void probe(file).then((durationSec) => setPicked((p) => (p?.file === file ? { file, durationSec } : p)))
  }
  const startRecording = () => {
    submit.clear()
    void recorder.start()
  }

  const recordedSec = rec.phase === 'recording' ? rec.elapsedSec : rec.phase === 'done' ? rec.recording.durationSec : 0
  const tooShort = tab === 'record' && rec.phase === 'done' && recordedSec < MIN_RECORDING_SEC
  const ready = tab === 'file' ? picked !== null : (rec.phase === 'recording' || rec.phase === 'done') && recordedSec >= MIN_RECORDING_SEC
  const sending = submit.state.status === 'sending'
  const error = submit.state.status === 'error' ? submit.state : null

  const sendRecording = (take: Recording) =>
    submit.send({ audio: take.blob, filename: take.filename, title: defaultTitle({ kind: 'recording', at: take.startedAt }, lang), mode, source: 'recording' })

  const send = async () => {
    if (tab === 'file') {
      if (picked) {
        const { file } = picked
        await submit.send({ audio: file, filename: file.name, title: defaultTitle({ kind: 'file', name: file.name }, lang), mode, source: 'file' })
      }
      return
    }
    // "Done" while recording stops the take and sends it in one tap, as in the mockup.
    const take = rec.phase === 'done' ? rec.recording : await recorder.stop()
    if (take && take.durationSec >= MIN_RECORDING_SEC) await sendRecording(take)
  }

  return (
    <div className="screen new-song">
      <header className="screen-head">
        <Link to="/" className="icon-btn" aria-label={m.common.close}><Close /></Link>
        <h1 className="screen-title">{m.newSong.title}</h1>
      </header>
      <div className="tabs" role="group" aria-label={m.newSong.source}>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={tab === 'record'} onClick={() => switchTab('record')}>{m.newSong.record}</button>
        <button type="button" aria-pressed={tab === 'file'} onClick={() => switchTab('file')}>{m.newSong.file}</button>
      </div>
      {tab === 'file' && <FilePanel picked={picked} onPick={pick} />}
      {tab === 'record' && rec.phase === 'error' && (
        <MicCard code={rec.code} onRetry={startRecording} onUpload={() => switchTab('file')} />
      )}
      {tab === 'record' && rec.phase !== 'error' && (
        <RecordPanel state={rec} onStart={startRecording} onStop={() => void recorder.stop()} />
      )}
      {tooShort && <p className="form-warn" role="status">{m.newSong.tooShort}</p>}
      <ModeChips value={mode} onChange={setMode} />
      {tab === 'record' && rec.phase !== 'error' && <RecordTip />}
      {error && (
        <div className="form-error" role="alert">
          <span>{errorText(m, error.code, error.message)}</span>
          {error.code === 'network' && <button type="button" className="text-btn" onClick={() => void send()}>{m.common.retry}</button>}
        </div>
      )}
      <button type="button" className="cta" disabled={!ready || sending} onClick={() => void send()}>
        {sending ? m.newSong.sending : m.newSong.submit}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

export function NewSongScreen() {
  const api = useMemo(() => getApi(), [])
  return api ? <NewSong api={api} /> : <Navigate to="/" replace />
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `stepStates`**

Create `app/src/processing/steps.ts`:

```ts
import type { JobStatus } from '../api/types'

export type StepId = 'separating' | 'analyzing' | 'melody' | 'arranging'
export type StepState = 'done' | 'active' | 'pending'

export const STEPS: StepId[] = ['separating', 'analyzing', 'melody', 'arranging']

export function stepStates(status: JobStatus): { id: StepId; state: StepState }[] {
  const current = status === 'done' ? STEPS.length : status === 'queued' || status === 'failed' ? -1 : STEPS.indexOf(status)
  return STEPS.map((id, i) => ({ id, state: i < current ? 'done' : i === current ? 'active' : 'pending' }))
}
```

Create `app/src/processing/GhostRoll.tsx`:

```tsx
// Note positions from the approved mockup, measured on its 342 px wide roll.
const NOTES = [
  [14, 20, 40], [34, 70, 30], [58, 10, 50], [80, 60, 44], [104, 30, 24], [128, 84, 38], [150, 14, 46],
  [176, 56, 52], [200, 24, 30], [222, 90, 36], [246, 40, 44], [270, 8, 30], [292, 70, 40], [316, 30, 50],
] as const
const WIDTH = 342

export function GhostRoll({ progress }: { progress: number }) {
  const line = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.04, progress))
  return (
    <div className="ghost-roll" aria-hidden="true">
      {NOTES.map(([x, y, h], i) => (
        <span
          key={i}
          className={`ghost-note ${i % 3 === 0 ? 'left' : 'right'}${x / WIDTH < line ? ' lit' : ''}`}
          style={{ left: `${(x / WIDTH) * 100}%`, top: y, height: h }}
        />
      ))}
      <span className="ghost-line" style={{ width: `${line * 100}%` }} />
    </div>
  )
}
```

Create `app/src/processing/StepList.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { JobPreview, JobStatus } from '../api/types'
import { useLang, useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { keyName } from '../i18n/music'
import { Check } from '../ui/icons'
import { stepStates } from './steps'

export function StepList({ status, preview }: { status: JobStatus; preview?: JobPreview }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const lang = useLang((s) => s.lang)
  return (
    <ol className="steps">
      {stepStates(status).map(({ id, state }) => {
        const detail =
          id === 'analyzing' && preview
            ? `${keyName(preview.key.tonic, preview.key.mode, lang)} · ${preview.timeSignature[0]}/${preview.timeSignature[1]}`
            : m.processing.steps[id].detail
        return (
          <li key={id} className={`step ${state}`} aria-current={state === 'active' ? 'step' : undefined}>
            <span className="step-dot" aria-hidden="true">{state === 'done' && <Check />}</span>
            <span className="step-body">
              <span className="step-label">
                {m.processing.steps[id].label}
                <span className="sr-only"> · {m.processing.stepState[state]}</span>
              </span>
              <span className="step-detail">{detail}</span>
              {state === 'active' && <span className="step-bar" aria-hidden="true"><span /></span>}
            </span>
          </li>
        )
      })}
    </ol>
  )
}
```

Create `app/src/processing/FoundSoFar.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { JobPreview } from '../api/types'
import { useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'

export function FoundSoFar({ preview }: { preview: JobPreview }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  return (
    <section className="found">
      <h2 className="field-label">{m.processing.found}</h2>
      <div className="found-row">
        {preview.chords.map((chord, i) => (
          <span key={`${chord}-${i}`} className={i === 0 ? 'found-chip first' : 'found-chip'}>{chord}</span>
        ))}
        <span className="found-bpm">{Math.round(preview.bpm)} BPM</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}
```

Create `app/src/processing/ProcessingScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
import { Link, Navigate, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router'
import { ApiError, getApi, type Api } from '../api/client'
import { errorText } from '../api/errorText'
import type { Job } from '../api/types'
import { usePoll } from '../api/usePoll'
import { useMessages } from '../i18n/lang'
import { formatTime } from '../player/timeline'
import { ChevronLeft } from '../ui/icons'
import { FoundSoFar } from './FoundSoFar'
import { GhostRoll } from './GhostRoll'
import { StepList } from './StepList'

export const POLL_MS = 2000

const finished = (job: Job | null) => job === null || job.status === 'done' || job.status === 'failed'

export function Processing({ api, jobId }: { api: Api; jobId: string }) {
  const m = useMessages()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  // A 404 resolves to null so polling stops; every other error keeps retrying.
  const poll = usePoll(
    () =>
      api.getJob(jobId).catch((e: unknown) => {
        if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === 'not_found') return null
        throw e
      }),
    POLL_MS,
    { stop: finished },
  )
  const job = poll.data

  useEffect(() => {
    if (job?.status === 'done' && job.songId) navigate(`/song/${encodeURIComponent(job.songId)}`, { replace: true })
  }, [job, navigate])

  if (job === null) {
    return (
      <div className="player-message">
        <p>{errorText(m, 'not_found')}</p>
        <Link to="/" className="pill-link"><ChevronLeft />{m.common.back}</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const failed = job?.status === 'failed'
  return (
    <div className="screen processing">
      <header className="proc-head">
        <Link to="/" className="icon-btn" aria-label={m.common.back}><ChevronLeft /></Link>
      </header>
      <div className="proc-intro">
        <p className="kicker">{job ? `${m.processing.source[job.source]} · ${formatTime(job.durationSec)}` : ' '}</p>
        <h1 className="proc-title">{failed ? m.processing.failedTitle : m.processing.title}</h1>
        {job && <p className="proc-sub">{job.title}</p>}
      </div>
      <GhostRoll progress={job?.progress ?? 0} />
      {job && failed ? (
        <div className="form-error" role="alert">
          <p>{errorText(m, job.error?.code ?? 'internal', job.error?.message)}</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <StepList status={job?.status ?? 'queued'} preview={job?.preview} />
          {job?.status === 'queued' && <p className="proc-note">{m.processing.queued}</p>}
          {job?.preview && <FoundSoFar preview={job.preview} />}
        </>
      )}
      {poll.error !== undefined && <p className="proc-note" role="status">{m.processing.reconnecting}</p>}
      {failed ? (
        <Link to="/new" className="cta">{m.common.retry}</Link>
      ) : (
        <Link to="/" className="ghost-cta">{m.processing.background}</Link>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function ProcessingScreen() {
  const { jobId = '' } = useParams()
  const api = useMemo(() => getApi(), [])
  return api && jobId ? <Processing key={jobId} api={api} jobId={jobId} /> : <Navigate to="/" replace />
}
```

```tsx
import { BrowserRouter, Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { LibraryScreen } from './library/LibraryScreen'
import { NewSongScreen } from './newsong/NewSongScreen'
import { PlayerScreen } from './player/PlayerScreen'
import { ProcessingScreen } from './processing/ProcessingScreen'

export function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<LibraryScreen />} />
        <Route path="/new" element={<NewSongScreen />} />
        <Route path="/processing/:jobId" element={<ProcessingScreen />} />
        <Route path="/song/:id" element={<PlayerScreen />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
      </Routes>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}
```

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test'

const DEMO = 'http://localhost:5174'
const API = 'http://localhost:5175'

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: 'e2e',
  use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'], viewport: { width: 844, height: 390 } },
  projects: [
    { name: 'demo', testMatch: /smoke\.spec\.ts$/, use: { baseURL: DEMO } },
    // Portrait phone: the New song and Processing screens are designed for it.
    { name: 'api', testMatch: /new-song\.spec\.ts$/, use: { baseURL: API, viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 } } },
  ],
  webServer: [
    // Environment variables beat .env files in Vite, so a local .env.local can't switch the demo to the API.
    { command: 'pnpm dev --port 5174 --strictPort', url: DEMO, reuseExistingServer: true, env: { VITE_API_URL: '', VITE_API_KEY: '' } },
    { command: 'pnpm dev --port 5175 --strictPort', url: API, reuseExistingServer: false, env: { VITE_API_URL: 'http://api.test', VITE_API_KEY: 'e2e-key' } },
  ],
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the API e2e spec**

Create `app/e2e/new-song.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { expect, test, type Page, type Route } from '@playwright/test'

const API_ORIGIN = 'http://api.test'
const demoSong = readFileSync(new URL('../public/songs/demo/song.json', import.meta.url), 'utf-8')
const demoSummary = { id: 'demo', title: 'Demo · Am F C G', key: { tonic: 'A', mode: 'minor' }, bpm: 90, durationSec: 22.3, sourceType: 'instrumental', chords: ['Am', 'F', 'C', 'G'] }
const job = { id: 'j1', title: 'Take Five', source: 'file', durationSec: 12, createdAt: '2026-11-09T12:00:00Z' }
const preview = { key: { tonic: 'A', mode: 'minor' }, bpm: 90, timeSignature: [4, 4], chords: ['Am', 'F', 'C', 'G'] }
const cors = { 'access-control-allow-origin': '*', 'access-control-allow-headers': 'authorization', 'access-control-allow-methods': 'GET, POST, DELETE' }

// 16-bit mono PCM silence: a real file the browser can read the length of.
function wav(seconds: number, rate = 8000): Buffer {
  const bytes = seconds * rate * 2
  const b = Buffer.alloc(44 + bytes)
  b.write('RIFF', 0)
  b.writeUInt32LE(36 + bytes, 4)
  b.write('WAVE', 8)
  b.write('fmt ', 12)
  b.writeUInt32LE(16, 16)
  b.writeUInt16LE(1, 20)
  b.writeUInt16LE(1, 22)
  b.writeUInt32LE(rate, 24)
  b.writeUInt32LE(rate * 2, 28)
  b.writeUInt16LE(2, 32)
  b.writeUInt16LE(16, 34)
  b.write('data', 36)
  b.writeUInt32LE(bytes, 40)
  return b
}

async function fakeServer(page: Page) {
  const state = { down: false, finish: false, uploadType: '' }
  await page.route((url) => url.origin === API_ORIGIN, async (route: Route) => {
    const req = route.request()
    if (state.down) return route.abort()
    if (req.method() === 'OPTIONS') return route.fulfill({ status: 204, headers: cors })
    const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
      route.fulfill({ status, headers: cors, contentType: 'application/json', body: JSON.stringify(body) })
    if (req.headers()['authorization'] !== 'Bearer e2e-key') return json({ error: { code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Bad key.' } }, 401)

    const url = new URL(req.url())
    const path = url.pathname + url.search
    if (path === '/api/songs') return json(state.finish ? [demoSummary] : [])
    if (path === '/api/jobs?active=1') return json([])
    if (path === '/api/jobs' && req.method() === 'POST') {
      state.uploadType = req.headers()['content-type'] ?? ''
      return json({ jobId: 'j1' }, 202)
    }
    if (path === '/api/jobs/j1') {
      return json(state.finish ? { ...job, status: 'done', progress: 1, songId: 'demo', preview } : { ...job, status: 'analyzing', progress: 0.35, preview })
    }
    if (path === '/api/songs/demo') return route.fulfill({ status: 200, headers: cors, contentType: 'application/json', body: demoSong })
    return json({ error: { code: 'not_found', message: 'Not found.' } }, 404)
  })
  return state
}

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  // The piano samples come from the network; the app must keep working without them.
  await page.route('https://tonejs.github.io/**', (route) => route.abort())
  await page.addInitScript(() => localStorage.setItem('pianoverse.lang', 'el'))
})

test('upload a file → processing → player → library', async ({ page }) => {
  const server = await fakeServer(page)
  await page.goto('/')
  await expect(page.getByText('Δεν υπάρχουν ακόμα κομμάτια.')).toBeVisible()

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Νέο' }).click()
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Νέο τραγούδι' })).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Από αρχείο' }).click()
  await page.getByLabel(/Διάλεξε αρχείο ήχου/).setInputFiles({ name: 'Take Five.wav', mimeType: 'audio/wav', buffer: wav(12) })
  await expect(page.getByText('Take Five.wav')).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText('0:12 · Άλλαξε αρχείο')).toBeVisible()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Instrumental' }).click()
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Τέλος — βγάλε μου πιάνο' }).click()

  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/processing\/j1$/)
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Φτιάχνω τη διασκευή σου' })).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText('Αρχείο · 0:12')).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText('Λα ελάσσονα · 4/4')).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText('90 BPM')).toBeVisible()
  expect(server.uploadType).toMatch(/^multipart\/form-data/)

  server.finish = true
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/song\/demo$/, { timeout: 10_000 })
  await expect(page.getByRole('img', { name: 'Νότες που πέφτουν προς το πληκτρολόγιο' })).toBeVisible()

  await page.goto('/')
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: /Demo · Am F C G/ })).toBeVisible()
})

test('the library says when the server is down and recovers on retry', async ({ page }) => {
  const server = await fakeServer(page)
  server.down = true
  await page.goto('/')
  await expect(page.getByText('Δεν βρίσκω τον server.')).toBeVisible()
  server.down = false
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Δοκίμασε ξανά' }).click()
  await expect(page.getByText('Δεν υπάρχουν ακόμα κομμάτια.')).toBeVisible()
  await expect(page.getByText('Δεν βρίσκω τον server.')).toBeHidden()
})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Document running against the backend**

In `app/README.md`, insert after the "Your own songs" section:

````markdown
## With the backend (New song)

"+ New", recording, upload and the processing screen need the engine's server (`engine/README.md`). Without `VITE_API_URL` the app stays the offline demo above.

```bash
cd engine && PIANOVERSE_API_KEY=choose-a-key uv run pianoverse-server   # http://127.0.0.1:8000
cd app && cp .env.example .env.local                                      # set VITE_API_KEY=choose-a-key
pnpm dev                                                                  # open http://localhost:5173
```

| Variable | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `VITE_API_URL` | Server base URL without `/api`, e.g. `http://127.0.0.1:8000` |
| `VITE_API_KEY` | Sent as `Authorization: Bearer …`; must equal the server's `PIANOVERSE_API_KEY` |

- Open `http://localhost:5173`, not `127.0.0.1:5173`: the server's default CORS origin is `http://localhost:5173`.
- The key ends up in the web bundle: fine for a personal tool, not for a public site.
- Recording needs a secure origin: `http://localhost` counts; a LAN address over plain http does not (use the deployed https site on a phone).
- Unit tests and the demo e2e ignore `.env.local`; `pnpm test:e2e` also runs the New-song flow against a mocked API (`e2e/new-song.spec.ts`).
````

```bash
pnpm test:e2e  # demo smoke test + New song flow with a mocked API (first: pnpm exec playwright install chromium)
```

The implementer cannot do this step. The controller relays it to the user (in Greek, step by step) and waits for the answers before Task 10. Nothing in it is committed.

**USER STEPS**

1. Terminal 1 — start the server with a key of your choice:
   ```bash
   cd ~/Code/athanasu/pianoverse/engine
   PIANOVERSE_API_KEY=dev-key uv run pianoverse-server
   ```
   Expected: a line like `Uvicorn running on http://127.0.0.1:8000`. In another terminal, `curl http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/health` prints `{"ok":true}`.
2. Terminal 2 — start the app pointed at it:
   ```bash
   cd ~/Code/athanasu/pianoverse/app
   printf 'VITE_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000\nVITE_API_KEY=dev-key\n' > .env.local
   pnpm dev
   ```
   Open **http://localhost:5173** in Chrome or Safari on the Mac (exactly `localhost`, not `127.0.0.1`).
3. Library: the orange "+ Νέο" button is at the bottom; the list is empty (or shows songs from earlier runs).
4. Upload: "+ Νέο" → "Από αρχείο" → choose a song you own (30 s – 8 min, mp3/m4a/wav) → its name and length appear → pick the right chip (e.g. "Τραγούδι") → "Τέλος — βγάλε μου πιάνο".
   Expected: the "Φτιάχνω τη διασκευή σου" screen; the steps tick over one by one with the orange bar moving on the active one; after "Βρίσκω ρυθμό και συγχορδίες" the key and time signature appear under it and "Βρήκα μέχρι τώρα" shows chords and BPM; at the end the player opens by itself and plays. On CPU this can take a few minutes; Terminal 1 shows the requests.
5. "Συνέχισε στο παρασκήνιο": start another upload, tap it right away → the library shows a card for the job with its stage and a thin bar; tapping the card goes back to the processing screen; when it finishes the card turns into a song.
6. Record: "+ Νέο" → "Ηχογράφηση" → tap the big orange button → allow the microphone → play music from a speaker or another phone for ~30 s → the timer runs and the bars move → "Τέλος — βγάλε μου πιάνο" → same processing → player.
7. Short take: record for ~5 s and stop → the "πολύ μικρή" warning appears and the white button stays disabled.
8. Blocked mic: in the browser's site settings set Microphone to "Block", reload, tap record → the card "Δεν έχω πρόσβαση στο μικρόφωνο" with "Ανέβασε αρχείο". Set it back to "Allow".
9. Server down: stop the server (Ctrl+C in Terminal 1), reload the library → "Δεν βρίσκω τον server." with "Δοκίμασε ξανά"; start the server again and tap it → the songs come back.
10. Language: switch to EN on the library and glance at "New song" and the processing screen — everything is in English.

**What to report back:** for each numbered item, OK or what you saw instead (a screenshot helps), plus how long step 4 took. If anything fails, the controller fixes it before Task 10 starts.

### Task 10: Supabase (Postgres Store + Storage Blobs)

Goal: the same server runs against Supabase. `Store` works on Postgres (session pooler, psycopg 3) and `SupabaseBlobs` talks to the Storage REST API. `app_from_env` picks `SupabaseBlobs` when `SUPABASE_URL` is set. The Store contract tests also run on Postgres when `PIANOVERSE_TEST_DATABASE_URL` is set, and are skipped otherwise, so fast tests stay offline.

- Local only, never committed: `engine/.env.local`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (Tasks 1–4): `Settings.from_env(env)` with fields `data_dir`, `database_url`, `supabase_url`, `supabase_service_key`, `runner`; `Store(database_url)` and its methods; `Blobs` protocol (`put_bytes`, `put_file`, `get_bytes`, `download`); `LocalBlobs(root)`; `api.create_app(settings, store, blobs, runner, *, probe=...)`; `api.app_from_env()`.
- Produces:
  - `store.normalize_database_url(url: str) -> str` (`postgres://…` and `postgresql://…` become `postgresql+psycopg://…`; anything else unchanged)
  - `Store.close() -> None` (disposes the SQLAlchemy engine; Task 11's Modal worker calls it)
  - `blobs.BlobStoreError(RuntimeError)` with attribute `status: int`
  - `blobs.SupabaseBlobs(url: str, service_key: str, bucket: str = "pianoverse", client: httpx.Client | None = None)` implementing `Blobs`
  - `blobs.make_blobs(settings: Settings) -> Blobs` (`SupabaseBlobs` when `settings.supabase_url` is set, else `LocalBlobs` exactly as `app_from_env` built it before)

Supabase specifics this task relies on:
- Storage REST: upload `POST {SUPABASE_URL}/storage/v1/object/{bucket}/{key}` with header `x-upsert: true` (overwrite allowed) and the object's `Content-Type`; download `GET {SUPABASE_URL}/storage/v1/object/authenticated/{bucket}/{key}` (the explicit private-bucket route).
- Keys: new projects have **secret keys** (`sb_secret_…`), which are not JWTs and must be sent only in the `apikey` header. Legacy `service_role` keys are JWTs (`eyJ…`) and are sent as both `apikey` and `Authorization: Bearer`. `SupabaseBlobs` supports both.
- A missing object comes back as HTTP 404, or (older Storage versions) HTTP 400 with body `{"statusCode": "404", "error": "not_found", …}`. Both mean `KeyError`.
- Postgres: use the **session pooler** connection string (port 5432, IPv4, supports prepared statements). The direct connection is IPv6-only and the transaction pooler breaks psycopg's prepared statements.

```python
from pathlib import Path

import httpx
import pytest

from pianoverse_engine.server.blobs import (
    BlobStoreError,
    LocalBlobs,
    SupabaseBlobs,
    make_blobs,
)
from pianoverse_engine.server.config import Settings

URL = "https://abcd1234.supabase.co"
LEGACY_KEY = "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiJ9.legacy.signature"
SECRET_KEY = "sb_secret_test_0123456789"
UPLOAD_PREFIX = "/storage/v1/object/pianoverse/"
DOWNLOAD_PREFIX = "/storage/v1/object/authenticated/pianoverse/"


class FakeStorage:
    """In-memory stand-in for Supabase Storage, served through httpx.MockTransport."""

    def __init__(self, missing_status: int = 400) -> None:
        self.objects: dict[str, tuple[bytes, str]] = {}
        self.requests: list[httpx.Request] = []
        self.missing_status = missing_status
        self.fail_with: int | None = None

    def handler(self, request: httpx.Request) -> httpx.Response:
        self.requests.append(request)
        if self.fail_with is not None:
            return httpx.Response(
                self.fail_with,
                json={"statusCode": str(self.fail_with), "error": "internal", "message": "storage is down"},
            )
        path = request.url.path
        if request.method == "POST" and path.startswith(UPLOAD_PREFIX):
            key = path[len(UPLOAD_PREFIX):]
            self.objects[key] = (request.content, request.headers["content-type"])
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"Key": f"pianoverse/{key}"})
        if request.method == "GET" and path.startswith(DOWNLOAD_PREFIX):
            key = path[len(DOWNLOAD_PREFIX):]
            if key not in self.objects:
                return httpx.Response(
                    self.missing_status,
                    json={"statusCode": "404", "error": "not_found", "message": "Object not found"},
                )
            data, content_type = self.objects[key]
            return httpx.Response(200, content=data, headers={"content-type": content_type})
        return httpx.Response(405, json={"message": "unexpected request"})


def make(storage: FakeStorage, key: str = LEGACY_KEY) -> SupabaseBlobs:
    client = httpx.Client(transport=httpx.MockTransport(storage.handler))
    return SupabaseBlobs(URL, key, client=client)


def test_put_bytes_posts_to_the_bucket_with_upsert_and_legacy_key_headers():
    storage = FakeStorage()
    make(storage).put_bytes("songs/abc/song.json", b'{"id": "abc"}', "application/json")

    request = storage.requests[-1]
    assert request.method == "POST"
    assert str(request.url) == f"{URL}/storage/v1/object/pianoverse/songs/abc/song.json"
    assert request.headers["x-upsert"] == "true"
    assert request.headers["content-type"] == "application/json"
    assert request.headers["apikey"] == LEGACY_KEY
    assert request.headers["authorization"] == f"Bearer {LEGACY_KEY}"
    assert storage.objects["songs/abc/song.json"] == (b'{"id": "abc"}', "application/json")
```

Append to `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/blobs.py` (merge the imports into the file's existing import block; keep `from __future__ import annotations` if the file already has it):

```python
import json
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
from urllib.parse import quote

import httpx

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .config import Settings


class BlobStoreError(RuntimeError):
    """A blob request failed for a reason other than a missing key."""

    def __init__(self, status: int, detail: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"blob store request failed with HTTP {status}: {detail}")
        self.status = status


def _quoted_key(key: str) -> str:
    """Validate a blob key ("songs/abc/song.json") and percent-encode it for a URL path."""
    parts = key.split("/")
    if not key or any(part in ("", ".", "..") for part in parts):
        raise ValueError(f"invalid blob key: {key!r}")
    return quote(key, safe="/")


def _is_missing(response: httpx.Response) -> bool:
    """Supabase answers 404, or (older Storage versions) 400 with a body statusCode of "404"."""
    if response.status_code == 404:
        return True
    if response.status_code != 400:
        return False
    try:
        body = json.loads(response.content)
    except ValueError:
        return False
    return isinstance(body, dict) and (str(body.get("statusCode")) == "404" or body.get("error") == "not_found")


def _raise_for_status(response: httpx.Response) -> None:
    if response.status_code >= 400:
        raise BlobStoreError(response.status_code, response.text[:300])


class SupabaseBlobs:
    """Blobs in a private Supabase Storage bucket, through the Storage REST API."""

    def __init__(self, url: str, service_key: str, bucket: str = "pianoverse",
                 client: httpx.Client | None = None) -> None:
        if not url or not service_key:
            raise ValueError("SupabaseBlobs needs the project URL and a secret/service key")
        base = url.rstrip("/")
        self._upload_base = f"{base}/storage/v1/object/{bucket}"
        self._download_base = f"{base}/storage/v1/object/authenticated/{bucket}"
        # New secret keys (sb_secret_...) are not JWTs and go only in `apikey`;
        # legacy service_role keys are JWTs and also go in Authorization.
        self._auth = {"apikey": service_key}
        if not service_key.startswith("sb_"):
            self._auth["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {service_key}"
        self._client = client if client is not None else httpx.Client(
            timeout=httpx.Timeout(120.0, connect=10.0))

    def put_bytes(self, key: str, data: bytes, content_type: str) -> None:
        response = self._client.post(
            f"{self._upload_base}/{_quoted_key(key)}",
            content=data,
            headers={**self._auth, "Content-Type": content_type, "x-upsert": "true"},
        )
        _raise_for_status(response)

    def put_file(self, key: str, path: Path, content_type: str) -> None:
        # Uploads are capped at 50 MB by the API, so reading the file whole is fine.
        self.put_bytes(key, path.read_bytes(), content_type)

    def get_bytes(self, key: str) -> bytes:
        response = self._client.get(f"{self._download_base}/{_quoted_key(key)}", headers=self._auth)
        if _is_missing(response):
            raise KeyError(key)
        _raise_for_status(response)
        return response.content

    def download(self, key: str, dest: Path) -> None:
        url = f"{self._download_base}/{_quoted_key(key)}"
        dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        part = dest.with_name(dest.name + ".part")
        with self._client.stream("GET", url, headers=self._auth) as response:
            if response.status_code >= 400:
                response.read()
                if _is_missing(response):
                    raise KeyError(key)
                _raise_for_status(response)
            try:
                with part.open("wb") as f:
                    for chunk in response.iter_bytes():
                        f.write(chunk)
            except BaseException:
                part.unlink(missing_ok=True)
                raise
        part.replace(dest)


def make_blobs(settings: "Settings") -> "Blobs":
    """Supabase Storage when SUPABASE_URL is set, else the local folder."""
    if settings.supabase_url:
        if not settings.supabase_service_key:
            raise ValueError("SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY is required when SUPABASE_URL is set")
        return SupabaseBlobs(settings.supabase_url, settings.supabase_service_key)
    return LocalBlobs(settings.data_dir / "blobs")
```

`Path` and `Blobs` already exist in `blobs.py` (Task 2); if `Path` is not imported there yet, add `from pathlib import Path`.

- [ ] **Step 8: Make the Store contract tests run on Postgres too**

Look at how `tests/test_store.py` builds stores:

```bash
grep -n "Store(\|def store\|@pytest.fixture" tests/test_store.py
```

Replace the existing SQLite store fixture (or, if tests call `Store(f"sqlite:///...")` inline, replace those calls with the `store` fixture argument) with the block below, placed right after the imports. Every contract test that receives `store` then runs twice: `[sqlite]` always, `[postgres]` only when `PIANOVERSE_TEST_DATABASE_URL` is set. Postgres runs use a dedicated `pianoverse_test` schema, so they never touch the real `public.jobs` / `public.songs` tables, even when the URL points at the production database.

```python
import os

import sqlalchemy as sa

from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store, normalize_database_url

TEST_SCHEMA = "pianoverse_test"


def _use_test_schema(dbapi_connection, connection_record) -> None:
    with dbapi_connection.cursor() as cursor:
        cursor.execute(f"SET search_path TO {TEST_SCHEMA}")
    dbapi_connection.commit()


@pytest.fixture(params=["sqlite", "postgres"])
def database_url(request: pytest.FixtureRequest, tmp_path):
    if request.param == "sqlite":
        yield f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'store.db'}"
        return
    url = os.environ.get("PIANOVERSE_TEST_DATABASE_URL")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("set PIANOVERSE_TEST_DATABASE_URL to run the Store contract tests on Postgres")
    url = normalize_database_url(url)
    admin = sa.create_engine(url)
    with admin.begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(sa.text(f"CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS {TEST_SCHEMA}"))
        conn.execute(sa.text(f"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {TEST_SCHEMA}.jobs, {TEST_SCHEMA}.songs"))
    admin.dispose()
    sa.event.listen(sa.engine.Engine, "connect", _use_test_schema)
    try:
        yield url
    finally:
        sa.event.remove(sa.engine.Engine, "connect", _use_test_schema)


@pytest.fixture
def store(database_url: str):
    s = Store(database_url)
    yield s
    s.close()
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize(("given", "expected"), [
    ("postgresql://u:p@host:5432/postgres", "postgresql+psycopg://u:p@host:5432/postgres"),
    ("postgres://u:p@host:5432/postgres", "postgresql+psycopg://u:p@host:5432/postgres"),
    ("postgresql+psycopg://u:p@host/db", "postgresql+psycopg://u:p@host/db"),
    ("sqlite:///data/pianoverse.db", "sqlite:///data/pianoverse.db"),
])
def test_normalize_database_url(given: str, expected: str):
    assert normalize_database_url(given) == expected


def test_store_can_be_closed_and_reopened(database_url: str):
    first = Store(database_url)
    job = first.create_job(title="Close me", mode="auto", source="file", duration_sec=30.0,
                           audio_key="audio/x.wav")
    first.close()
    second = Store(database_url)
    try:
        assert second.get_job(job.id) is not None
    finally:

def test_postgres_tables_have_row_level_security(database_url: str):
    if not database_url.startswith("postgresql"):
        pytest.skip("Postgres only")
    Store(database_url).close()
    engine = sa.create_engine(database_url)
    try:
        with engine.connect() as conn:
            rows = conn.execute(sa.text(
                "SELECT relname, relrowsecurity FROM pg_class "
                "WHERE relnamespace = CAST(:schema AS regnamespace) AND relname IN ('jobs', 'songs')"
            ), {"schema": TEST_SCHEMA}).all()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
    assert dict(rows) == {"jobs": True, "songs": True}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implement the Store changes**

In `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/store.py`, add near the top (after the imports; add `text` and `Engine` to the SQLAlchemy imports if missing):

```python
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

_POSTGRES_PREFIXES = ("postgres://", "postgresql://")
_RLS_TABLES = ("jobs", "songs")


def normalize_database_url(url: str) -> str:
    """Point bare Postgres URLs (as Supabase shows them) at the psycopg 3 driver."""
    for prefix in _POSTGRES_PREFIXES:
        if url.startswith(prefix):
            return "postgresql+psycopg://" + url[len(prefix):]
    return url


def _enable_row_level_security(engine: Engine) -> None:
    """Supabase exposes the public schema through its Data API; RLS with no policies closes that door.

    The server connects as the table owner, which RLS does not restrict.
    """
    if engine.dialect.name != "postgresql":
        return
    with engine.begin() as conn:
        for table in _RLS_TABLES:
            conn.execute(text(f"ALTER TABLE {table} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY"))
```

In `Store.__init__`, change the engine creation and add the RLS call right after `metadata.create_all(...)`. Task 2 names the engine attribute `self._engine` and passes `check_same_thread=False` for SQLite (the LocalRunner thread shares the store); keep both:

```python
        url = normalize_database_url(database_url)
        connect_args = {"check_same_thread": False} if url.startswith("sqlite") else {}
        self._engine = create_engine(url, connect_args=connect_args, pool_pre_ping=True)
        metadata.create_all(self._engine)
        _enable_row_level_security(self._engine)
```

```python
        """Release pooled connections (Postgres poolers limit how many a project may hold)."""
        self._engine.dispose()
```

- [ ] **Step 12: USER STEPS — create the Supabase project (the implementer cannot do this; the controller relays it)**

> **USER STEPS — Supabase (about 10 minutes, free)**
>
> 1. Open https://supabase.com and click **Start your project**. Sign up (GitHub login is the fastest) and confirm your email if asked.
> 2. Create an organization if asked (name: anything, e.g. your name; plan: **Free**).
> 3. Click **New project**:
>    - Name: `pianoverse`
>    - Database password: click **Generate a password**, then make sure it has **only letters and digits** (no `@ : / ? #` — they break the connection string). Save it in your password manager.
>    - Region: the closest to you (e.g. **Central EU (Frankfurt)**).
>    - Leave the other options at their defaults and click **Create new project**. Wait 1–2 minutes until the dashboard is ready.
> 4. Create the private bucket: left menu **Storage** → **New bucket** → Name `pianoverse` → keep **Public bucket OFF** → (optional) turn on **Restrict file upload size** and set **50 MB** → **Save**.
> 5. Copy the **secret key**: left menu **Project Settings** → **API Keys**. Under **Secret keys**, copy the key that starts with `sb_secret_` (click the eye/copy icon; if there is none, click **Add new secret key**, name it `server`). If your project only shows **Legacy API keys**, copy the `service_role` key instead. Treat this key like a password.
> 6. Copy the **project URL**: it is `https://<project-ref>.supabase.co`, where `<project-ref>` is the code in your browser's address bar after `/project/` (e.g. `https://supabase.com/dashboard/project/abcd1234efgh` → `https://abcd1234efgh.supabase.co`). It is also shown under **Project Settings → Data API**.
> 7. Copy the **database connection string**: click **Connect** at the top of the dashboard → tab **Connection String** → Type **URI** → Method **Session pooler**. Copy the string (it looks like `postgresql://postgres.abcd1234efgh:[YOUR-PASSWORD]@aws-0-eu-central-1.pooler.supabase.com:5432/postgres`) and replace `[YOUR-PASSWORD]` (including the brackets) with the password from step 3.
> 8. Open the file `engine/.env.local` (Claude has created it with empty values) and fill in the three values, so it looks like this (with your real values, no quotes, no spaces around `=`):
>
>    ```
>    SUPABASE_URL=https://abcd1234efgh.supabase.co
>    SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY=sb_secret_...
>    PIANOVERSE_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres.abcd1234efgh:YourPassword123@aws-0-eu-central-1.pooler.supabase.com:5432/postgres
>    PIANOVERSE_TEST_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://postgres.abcd1234efgh:YourPassword123@aws-0-eu-central-1.pooler.supabase.com:5432/postgres
>    ```
>
>    (The last two lines are the same string. The tests use a separate `pianoverse_test` schema, so they never touch your songs.)
> 9. Save the file and tell Claude "done". Never paste these values into chat or commit them; `engine/.env.local` is ignored by git.

- [ ] **Step 13: Verify the Store contract on real Postgres**

After the user says the file is filled in (from `engine/`; `uv run --env-file` loads the file without echoing it):

```bash
grep -cE '=.+' .env.local   # expect 4: every line has a value
uv run --env-file .env.local pytest tests/test_store.py -v
```

Expected: every contract test appears twice and both `[sqlite]` and `[postgres]` PASS; `test_postgres_tables_have_row_level_security[postgres]` PASSES; no `[postgres]` case is SKIPPED.

If a `[postgres]` case fails while `[sqlite]` passes, fix `store.py` (not the test). Usual causes:

| Failure | Fix in `store.py` |
|---|---|
| `can't compare offset-naive and offset-aware datetimes`, or timestamps off by the UTC offset | Declare timestamp columns `DateTime(timezone=True)` and normalize on read with `dt if dt.tzinfo else dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)` in the row→record mappers |
| Order of `list_songs()` / `list_active_jobs()` differs | Add an explicit tie-breaker: `.order_by(table.c.created_at.desc(), table.c.id.desc())` |
| `value too long for type character varying(N)` | Use `String` without a length (SQLite ignored it, Postgres does not) |
| `psycopg.OperationalError: connection ... failed` | The URL is not the **session pooler** one, or the password contains special characters; redo USER STEP 7 |

Re-run the command until all PASS.

### Task 11: Modal (API + GPU worker) and Cloudflare Pages

Goal: the API runs on Modal (CPU, scale-to-zero) with `PIANOVERSE_RUNNER=modal`; each job runs in a Modal GPU function `process_job`; model weights are baked into the image at build time; the app is served over HTTPS from Cloudflare Pages, so recording works from a phone browser.

**Files:**
- Modify: `engine/pyproject.toml` / `engine/uv.lock` (`modal` in a `cloud` dependency group, default groups)
- Modify: `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/separate.py` (`DEMUCS_MODEL` constant)
- Modify: `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/beats.py` (`BEAT_THIS_CHECKPOINT` constant)
- Create: `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/warmup.py` (`warm_models()`)
- Modify: `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/runner.py` (`ModalRunner`, `make_runner`)
- Modify: `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/api.py` (`app_from_env` uses `make_runner`; local-only startup work guarded)
- Create: `engine/modal_app.py`
- Create: `engine/tests/test_warmup.py`, `engine/tests/test_runner_modal.py`
- Modify: `engine/tests/test_app_from_env.py`
- Modify: `app/package.json` (`deploy` script)
- Modify: `engine/README.md`, `README.md`, `docs/HANDOVER.md`
- Local only, never committed: `engine/.env.modal`, `app/.env.production.local`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Settings.from_env()` (`runner: Literal["local","modal"]`, `api_key`, `database_url`); `Store(database_url)`, `Store.close()`, `Store.create_job/update_job/get_job` (Task 10); `make_blobs(settings)` (Task 10); `LocalRunner(store, blobs, *, run=run_pipeline)` with `start()`/`stop()`; `Runner` protocol `submit(job_id: str) -> None`; `worker.process_job(job_id, store, blobs, *, run=run_pipeline, workdir_root=None)`; `api.app_from_env()`; `chords_btc.ensure_weights()`.
- Produces:
  - `separate.DEMUCS_MODEL = "htdemucs_6s"`, `beats.BEAT_THIS_CHECKPOINT = "final0"`
  - `warmup.Loader = Callable[[], object]`, `warmup.default_loaders() -> list[Loader]`, `warmup.warm_models(loaders: Sequence[Loader] | None = None) -> None`
  - `runner.ModalRunner(spawn: Callable[[str], object] | None = None, *, app_name: str = "pianoverse", function_name: str = "process_job")` implementing `Runner`
  - `runner.make_runner(settings: Settings, store: Store, blobs: Blobs) -> Runner` (`ModalRunner()` when `settings.runner == "modal"`, else `LocalRunner(store, blobs)`, not started)
  - Modal app `pianoverse` with functions `process_job(job_id: str)` (GPU) and `api` (ASGI); secret `pianoverse-secrets`

Choices:
- **GPU: T4.** Only Demucs, beat_this and the ByteDance piano model use the GPU; BTC, Basic Pitch (TensorFlow, CPU-only here), librosa and the arranger are CPU-bound, so a faster GPU (L4/A10G) shortens a job only a little but costs 35–80 % more per second. T4 (16 GB) fits `htdemucs_6s` on an 8-minute song. With `cpu=4`, `memory=8192` and a ~2-minute job this is about $0.02–0.03 per song, which matches spec §4.3. Switch to `gpu="L4"` only if jobs routinely take longer than 5 minutes.
- **Retries:** `retries=1` on `process_job`. `worker.process_job` already turns every Python exception into a `failed` job, so Modal retries only on infrastructure failures (container crash, out-of-memory, preemption, timeout). If the retry also dies, the job stays in its last stage and the 20-minute stale rule marks it `interrupted` when it is read.
- **No `fail_interrupted()` at API startup on Modal.** API containers start and stop all the time while GPU jobs are running; failing "interrupted" jobs at startup would kill live jobs. Only the local runner does it.
- **Weights at build time:** `warm_models()` runs in `Image.run_function`, after the engine source is copied into the image (`copy=True`), so any engine code change re-runs it on the next deploy (about 1–3 extra minutes to re-download ~500 MB). That keeps a single, simple image.
- **SPA fallback on Pages:** Cloudflare Pages serves `index.html` for unknown paths when the build has no top-level `404.html`, and Wrangler rejects `/* /index.html 200` in `_redirects` as an infinite loop. So no `_redirects` file; Step 14 checks that deep links like `/song/<id>` load.
