Create `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/warmup.py`:

```python
"""Fetch every model checkpoint into its cache, so a fresh machine (or image build) needs no downloads later.

Used by engine/modal_app.py at image build time; also handy on a new Mac: `uv run python -m pianoverse_engine.warmup`.
"""
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence

Loader = Callable[[], object]


def demucs() -> object:
    from demucs.pretrained import get_model

    from .separate import DEMUCS_MODEL

    return get_model(DEMUCS_MODEL)   # -> ~/.cache/torch/hub/checkpoints/


def beat_this() -> object:
    from beat_this.inference import load_checkpoint

    from .beats import BEAT_THIS_CHECKPOINT

    return load_checkpoint(BEAT_THIS_CHECKPOINT)   # -> ~/.cache/torch/hub/checkpoints/


def btc() -> object:
    from .chords_btc import ensure_weights

    return ensure_weights()   # -> ~/.cache/pianoverse/, SHA-256 checked


def basic_pitch() -> object:
    # Weights ship inside the package; loading proves the platform backend (TensorFlow on Linux) works.
    from basic_pitch import ICASSP_2022_MODEL_PATH
    from basic_pitch.inference import Model

    return Model(ICASSP_2022_MODEL_PATH)


def piano() -> object:
    # Downloads with `wget` to ~/piano_transcription_inference_data/ and loads it (raises if the file is bad).
    from piano_transcription_inference import PianoTranscription

    return PianoTranscription(device="cpu", checkpoint_path=None)


def default_loaders() -> list[Loader]:
    return [demucs, beat_this, btc, basic_pitch, piano]


def warm_models(loaders: Sequence[Loader] | None = None) -> None:
    for loader in loaders if loaders is not None else default_loaders():
        print(f"warm-up: {getattr(loader, '__name__', 'loader')}", flush=True)
        loader()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    warm_models()
```

```python
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from pianoverse_engine.server.config import Settings
from pianoverse_engine.server.runner import LocalRunner, ModalRunner, make_runner
from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store
from pianoverse_engine.server.blobs import LocalBlobs


def test_modal_runner_spawns_one_call_per_job():
    spawned: list[str] = []
    runner = ModalRunner(spawn=spawned.append)
    runner.submit("abc123")
    runner.submit("def456")
    assert spawned == ["abc123", "def456"]


def test_modal_runner_looks_up_the_deployed_function_lazily_and_once(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch):
    calls: list[tuple[str, str]] = []

    class FakeFunction:
        @staticmethod
        def from_name(app_name: str, name: str):
            calls.append(("from_name", f"{app_name}/{name}"))
            return SimpleNamespace(spawn=lambda job_id: calls.append(("spawn", job_id)))

    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "modal", SimpleNamespace(Function=FakeFunction))
    runner = ModalRunner()
    assert calls == []
    runner.submit("j1")
    runner.submit("j2")
    assert calls == [("from_name", "pianoverse/process_job"), ("spawn", "j1"), ("spawn", "j2")]


def test_modal_runner_lets_spawn_failures_through():
    def broken(job_id: str) -> None:
        raise ConnectionError("modal unreachable")

    with pytest.raises(ConnectionError):
        ModalRunner(spawn=broken).submit("j1")


def _settings(tmp_path: Path, runner: str) -> Settings:
    return Settings.from_env({"PIANOVERSE_DATA_DIR": str(tmp_path), "PIANOVERSE_RUNNER": runner})


def test_make_runner_picks_modal(tmp_path: Path):
    settings = _settings(tmp_path, "modal")
    store = Store(settings.database_url)
    try:
        assert isinstance(make_runner(settings, store, LocalBlobs(tmp_path / "blobs")), ModalRunner)
    finally:
        store.close()


def test_make_runner_defaults_to_an_unstarted_local_runner(tmp_path: Path):
    settings = _settings(tmp_path, "local")
    store = Store(settings.database_url)
    try:
        runner = make_runner(settings, store, LocalBlobs(tmp_path / "blobs"))
        assert isinstance(runner, LocalRunner)
    finally:
        store.close()
```

```python
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from pianoverse_engine.server.runner import ModalRunner
from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store


def _job_in_progress(database_url: str) -> str:
    store = Store(database_url)
    job = store.create_job(title="In flight", mode="auto", source="file", duration_sec=30.0,
                           audio_key="audio/x.wav")
    store.update_job(job.id, status="melody", progress=0.6)
    store.close()
    return job.id


def test_modal_runner_startup_leaves_in_flight_jobs_alone(clean_env, tmp_path):

    clean_env.setenv("PIANOVERSE_DATABASE_URL", url)
    clean_env.setenv("PIANOVERSE_RUNNER", "modal")

    with TestClient(api.app_from_env()):

    store = Store(url)
    assert store.get_job(job_id).status == "melody"
    store.close()


def test_local_runner_startup_still_fails_interrupted_jobs(clean_env, tmp_path):
    url = f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'pv.db'}"
    job_id = _job_in_progress(url)
    clean_env.setenv("PIANOVERSE_DATABASE_URL", url)

    job = store.get_job(job_id)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `ModalRunner` and `make_runner`**

Append to `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/runner.py` (merge imports with the existing ones):

```python
from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .blobs import Blobs
    from .config import Settings
    from .store import Store


class ModalRunner:
    """Runs each job in the deployed Modal GPU function (engine/modal_app.py: process_job)."""

    def __init__(self, spawn: Callable[[str], object] | None = None, *,
                 app_name: str = "pianoverse", function_name: str = "process_job") -> None:
        self._spawn = spawn
        self._app_name = app_name
        self._function_name = function_name

    def submit(self, job_id: str) -> None:
        if self._spawn is None:
            import modal   # only installed with the `cloud` group; imported on first use

            self._spawn = modal.Function.from_name(self._app_name, self._function_name).spawn
        self._spawn(job_id)


def make_runner(settings: "Settings", store: "Store", blobs: "Blobs") -> "Runner":
    """ModalRunner on Modal; otherwise the in-process LocalRunner (not started yet)."""
    if settings.runner == "modal":
        return ModalRunner()
    return LocalRunner(store, blobs)
```

If `runner.py` already imports `Store`/`Blobs` at runtime, drop them from the `TYPE_CHECKING` block.

- [ ] **Step 6: Use `make_runner` in `app_from_env` and guard local-only startup**

In `engine/src/pianoverse_engine/server/api.py`, import `make_runner` (keep `LocalRunner`) from `.runner`, and replace the whole `app_from_env` with the version below. It drops the Task 3 guard that rejected `PIANOVERSE_RUNNER=modal`, and runs the startup recovery and the worker thread only for the in-process runner:

```python
def app_from_env(env: Mapping[str, str] = os.environ) -> FastAPI:
    settings = Settings.from_env(env)
    settings.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    store = Store(settings.database_url)
    blobs = make_blobs(settings)
    runner = make_runner(settings, store, blobs)
    if not isinstance(runner, LocalRunner):
        # Other Modal containers may be running jobs right now, so nothing is failed or started here.
        return create_app(settings, store, blobs, runner)
    local = runner

    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
        store.fail_interrupted()   # the in-memory queue died with the previous process
        local.start()
        yield
        local.stop()

    return create_app(settings, store, blobs, runner, lifespan=lifespan)
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write `engine/modal_app.py`**

```python
"""Pianoverse on Modal: the FastAPI app (CPU, scale-to-zero) and the GPU job worker.

Deploy from engine/:   uv run modal deploy modal_app.py
Secrets (Modal secret "pianoverse-secrets"): PIANOVERSE_API_KEY, PIANOVERSE_DATABASE_URL,
SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY, PIANOVERSE_CORS_ORIGINS.
"""
import os

import modal

APP_NAME = "pianoverse"
SECRETS = [modal.Secret.from_name("pianoverse-secrets")]


def _warm_models() -> None:
    from pianoverse_engine.warmup import warm_models

    warm_models()


image = (
    modal.Image.debian_slim(python_version="3.11")
    .apt_install("ffmpeg", "wget")
    .uv_sync(uv_project_dir=".", groups=["cloud"])
    .env({
        "PIANOVERSE_RUNNER": "modal",
        "PIANOVERSE_DATA_DIR": "/tmp/pianoverse",
        # Basic Pitch's TensorFlow must not reserve the whole GPU that Demucs needs.
        "TF_FORCE_GPU_ALLOW_GROWTH": "true",
        "TF_CPP_MIN_LOG_LEVEL": "2",
    })
    .add_local_python_source("pianoverse_engine", copy=True)
    .run_function(_warm_models)
)

app = modal.App(APP_NAME, image=image)


@app.function(gpu="T4", cpu=4.0, memory=8192, timeout=900, retries=1, secrets=SECRETS)
def process_job(job_id: str) -> None:
    from pianoverse_engine.server.blobs import make_blobs
    from pianoverse_engine.server.config import Settings
    from pianoverse_engine.server.store import Store
    from pianoverse_engine.server.worker import process_job as run_job

    settings = Settings.from_env()
    store = Store(settings.database_url)
    try:
        run_job(job_id, store, make_blobs(settings))
    finally:
        store.close()


@app.function(secrets=SECRETS, timeout=300, scaledown_window=300)
@modal.concurrent(max_inputs=20)
@modal.asgi_app()
def api():
    if not os.environ.get("PIANOVERSE_API_KEY"):
        raise RuntimeError("PIANOVERSE_API_KEY is missing from the Modal secret pianoverse-secrets")
    from pianoverse_engine.server.api import app_from_env

    return app_from_env()
```

Smoke check (no network, no Modal account needed; it only builds the definitions):

```bash
uv run python -c "import modal_app; print(modal_app.app.name, sorted(modal_app.app.registered_functions))"
```

Expected: `pianoverse ['api', 'process_job']`.

In `app/package.json`, add to `"scripts"` (after `"build"`):

```json

```

Append to `engine/README.md`:

````markdown
## Cloud (Modal + Supabase)

The same server runs on [Modal](https://modal.com): `api` (FastAPI, CPU, scale-to-zero) and `process_job` (one GPU container per job, T4). Data lives in Supabase (Postgres + a private Storage bucket `pianoverse`). Model weights are downloaded into the image at build time (`pianoverse_engine/warmup.py`).

Secrets are kept in git-ignored files: `.env.local` (Supabase, for running locally against the cloud) and `.env.modal` (what the Modal secret holds).

```bash
uv run --env-file .env.local pianoverse-server          # local server, Supabase data
uv run --env-file .env.local pytest tests/test_store.py # Store contract on Postgres too
uv run modal secret create pianoverse-secrets --from-dotenv .env.modal --force
uv run modal deploy modal_app.py
uv run modal app logs pianoverse                        # follow logs
```

The web app is deployed from `app/` with `pnpm run deploy` (Cloudflare Pages; needs `app/.env.production.local` with `VITE_API_URL` and `VITE_API_KEY`).
````

```bash
cd /Users/georgios/Code/athanasu/pianoverse/engine
```

If the image build fails:
- `ModuleNotFoundError` for a third-party package inside `_warm_models`: the build did not use the uv environment. Replace the `.uv_sync(...)` line with an exported requirements file: run `uv export --frozen --no-hashes --no-emit-project --group cloud -o requirements-modal.txt`, change the image to `.uv_pip_install(requirements=["requirements-modal.txt"])`, and commit `requirements-modal.txt` (re-export whenever `uv.lock` changes).
- `warm-up: piano` fails with a checkpoint/`torch.load` error: the Zenodo download via `wget` failed or was cut short; run the deploy again.

```bash
cd /Users/georgios/Code/athanasu/pianoverse/engine
API_URL=https://<workspace>--pianoverse-api.modal.run   # from Step 12
KEY=$(grep '^PIANOVERSE_API_KEY=' .env.modal | cut -d= -f2-)

curl -s "$API_URL/api/health"
curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" "$API_URL/api/songs"

  | grep -i '^access-control-allow-origin'
```

Expected, in order: `{"ok":true}` (the first call may take ~10–30 s while a container starts), `401`, `200`, `access-control-allow-origin: https://pianoverse.pages.dev` (use your `PAGES_ORIGIN`).

Run one real GPU job from a sample file (any 20–60 s file in `engine/samples/`):

```bash
SAMPLE=$(ls samples/* | head -1)
JOB=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $KEY" -F "audio=@$SAMPLE" -F title="Cloud test" \
  -F mode=auto -F source=file "$API_URL/api/jobs" | uv run python -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin)["jobId"])')
for i in $(seq 1 60); do
  STATUS=$(curl -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $KEY" "$API_URL/api/jobs/$JOB" | uv run python -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(sys.stdin)["status"])')
  echo "$STATUS"; [ "$STATUS" = done ] || [ "$STATUS" = failed ] && break; sleep 10
done
```

Expected: statuses move through `queued`, `separating`, `analyzing`, `melody`, `arranging` and end at `done` within ~3 minutes (the first GPU cold start adds ~30–60 s). If it ends `failed` or stalls, read the logs with `uv run modal app logs pianoverse` and fix before continuing.

- [ ] **Step 14: Build and deploy the app to Cloudflare Pages**

```bash
cd /Users/georgios/Code/athanasu/pianoverse/app
API_URL=https://<workspace>--pianoverse-api.modal.run   # from Step 12, no trailing slash
KEY=$(grep '^PIANOVERSE_API_KEY=' ../engine/.env.modal | cut -d= -f2-)
printf 'VITE_API_URL=%s\nVITE_API_KEY=%s\n' "$API_URL" "$KEY" > .env.production.local
chmod 600 .env.production.local
git -C .. check-ignore -q app/.env.production.local && echo ignored
pnpm test && pnpm typecheck
pnpm run deploy
test ! -e dist/404.html && echo "spa fallback ok"
```

Expected: `ignored`; tests and typecheck pass; Wrangler uploads `dist/` and prints `Deployment complete!` with a URL; `spa fallback ok`. (Vite reads `.env.production.local` during `pnpm build`, so the Modal URL and key are baked into this build only.)
